TL;DR: 本文通过经典时间序列方法分析五个常用时空基准数据集（Chickenpox、PedalMe、WikiMaths、METR-LA、PEMS-BAY），发现空间无关的线性模型比此前报告的更具竞争力，揭示了一阶差分数据集引入的结构性偏差，并建议减少对这类数据集的过度依赖，同时提出一种基于统计分析的新型混合GNN模型开发方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前图神经网络（GNN）在多元时间序列短程预测中广泛使用，但评估过度依赖有限的基准数据集，且这些数据集常常难以区分GNN与简单线性模型的性能差异，需要对数据集本身进行更严格的统计分析。

Method: 采用经典时间序列分析方法对基准数据集进行统计检验，识别显著的空间和时间相关性，揭示一阶差分数据集引入的结构性偏差，并将分析结果应用于一个简单混合模型以验证方法论的可行性。

Result: 发现空间无关的线性模型在基准集上是比以往报告更强的竞争者；识别出数据集的空间与时间相关结构；证实一阶差分处理引入了结构性偏差，降低了基准集的判别可靠性。

Conclusion: 建议减少对现有基准数据集的过度依赖，倡导更严格的统计评估方法，并展示如何将统计分析与简单混合模型结合，为GNN模型发展提供新思路。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are routinely employed for short-range forecasting on multivariate time series with a spatial graph structure. Despite the availability of many alternative datasets, method innovations within this domain are predominantly assessed against a rather limited set of benchmark datasets, most notably Chickenpox, PedalMe, WikiMaths, METR-LA, and PEMS-BAY. The evaluation protocols contain baselines spanning from historical averages to classical machine learning approaches. These baselines often show competitive performance compared to GNNs. In the present work, we take a step back and analyse the benchmark datasets via classical time series methods to uncover why spatially-unaware linear models pose a stronger competitor than previously reported, casting further doubt on the discriminative reliability of the aforementioned widely adopted datasets. Our statistical analysis provides a toolset for identifying significant spatial and temporal correlations, while revealing a structural bias introduced by first-order differenced datasets. We therefore recommend reducing the over-reliance on such datasets for method comparison, and instead advocate for more rigorous statistical evaluation. By applying the results of our analysis to a simple hybrid model, we show how our methodology can lead to novel ways of developing GNN models

</details>


### [34] [Jacobian-guided Noise Injection for Quantization Robustness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20988)
*Deepanshu Pandey,Arnav Chavan,Nahush Lele,Sankalp Dayal,Deepak Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于雅可比矩阵引导的噪声注入训练策略（Jacobian-Guided Noise Injection），用于提升大语言模型在低比特量化下的鲁棒性。该方法通过向注意力层前的logits注入均值为零的高斯噪声，其方差由雅可比矩阵的Frobenius范数决定，从而有效抑制量化引起的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型量化常受限于自注意力机制对离散化误差的敏感性。现有研究识别出softmax算子因对异常值敏感和状态相关雅可比矩阵，成为量化稳定性的瓶颈。需要一种理论指导的训练策略来改善量化鲁棒性，而非依赖启发式方法。

Method: 首先从理论上证明抑制softmax雅可比矩阵的范数有助于限制量化引起的性能退化。基于此，提出雅可比引导噪声注入策略：在注意力logits前注入零均值高斯噪声，噪声方差直接由雅可比矩阵Frobenius范数推导得出，从而基于局部注意力敏感性识别最优噪声方差。

Result: 在SOTA LLM架构上的实验表明，该方法相比主流PTQ方法具有更好的鲁棒性。在SigLIP的ImageNet-1K上Top-1准确率相对提升最高达37%，在WikiText语言模型的低比特量化设置中相对困惑度改善最高达40%。

Conclusion: 该方法通过理论推导将噪声方差与注意力局部敏感性建立直接联系，避免了以往启发式或直接惩罚雅可比矩阵的做法，有效提升了LLM在低比特量化场景下的性能稳定性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Quantization of Large Language Models (LLMs) is often hindered by the sensitivity of the self-attention mechanism to discretization errors. We identify the softmax operator as a bottleneck for quantization stability due to its sensitivity to outliers and state-dependent Jacobian. We theoretically establish that suppressing the norm of this Jacobian helps in bounding quantization-induced performance degradation. Based on this, we propose Jacobian-Guided Noise Injection, a training strategy that injects zero-mean Gaussian noise into pre-attention logits, with variance derived directly from the Jacobian Frobenius norm. Unlike prior approaches that rely on heuristic or penalise jacobian directly, our method provides a way to identify the optimal noise variance based on the local attention sensitivity. We evaluate the method on SOTA LLM architectures, where it demonstrates improved robustness over popular PTQ methods. Empirical analysis reveals that the proposed method gives up to +37% relative gains on Top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1K for SigLIP and improves relative perplexity by upto 40% on WikiText for language models in low bit quantisation settings, proving the efficacy of the approach.

</details>


### [35] [Trojaning the Alignment: Stealthy Backdoor Attacks against Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20991)
*Minhua Lin,Zhicheng Gao,Yilong Wang,Hanqing Lu,Xiang Zhang,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STAG，一种针对文本属性图上的图基础模型（GFMs）图-语言对齐接口的隐蔽木马攻击框架。STAG通过协调图触发器生成器与文本侧软提示，使触发器附加的图表示和文本表示共同移向目标类别文本区域，同时通过候选检索实现触发器节点的可读文本，并正则化触发器附加子图保持局部结构接近原始子图，从而同时实现攻击有效性与隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击主要独立对待图和文本两个模态，图侧触发器受干净文本语义约束，文本侧触发器不直接改变被对齐和评分的图表示，导致直接迁移效果不佳；同时TAGs中触发器同时暴露为节点文本和局部图结构，不一致的触发器属性或异常子图容易被检测和过滤，隐蔽性面临挑战。

Method: STAG框架协调图触发器生成器与文本侧软提示，使触发器附加的图表示和触发文本表示同时朝目标类别文本区域移动；通过候选检索将触发节点实现为可读文本；对触发器附加子图进行正则化，使其局部结构保持接近原始子图，从而在多个TAG数据集和代表性GFMs上实现有效且隐蔽的攻击。

Result: 在多个文本属性图数据集和代表性图基础模型上的大量实验验证了STAG的有效性和隐蔽性。

Conclusion: STAG是针对GFMs在TAGs上图-语言对齐接口的首个隐蔽特洛伊攻击框架，填补了该领域后门安全研究的空白，代码已开源。

Abstract: Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) on text-attributed graphs (TAGs) align graph representations with language semantics to support transferable graph learning. Despite these advantages, the backdoor vulnerability of GFMs on TAGs remains insufficiently understood, especially under graph-language alignment, where graph and text representations are trained to constrain each other in a shared semantic space. Existing backdoor attacks mainly target either the graph side or the text side, treating the two modalities independently. This makes direct adaptation ineffective: graph-only triggers can be constrained by clean text semantics, while text-only triggers alter the language view but do not directly shift the graph representation being aligned and scored. TAGs also impose a stealth challenge because triggers are exposed as both node text and local graph structure, making incoherent trigger attributes or anomalous subgraphs easy to inspect or filter. In this paper, we propose STAG, a stealthy trojan attack framework designed for the graph-language alignment interface of GFMs on TAGs. STAG coordinates a graph-trigger generator with a text-side soft prompt so that trigger-attached graph representations and triggered text representations move toward the same target-class text region. To address TAG-specific stealthiness, STAG realizes trigger nodes as readable text through candidate retrieval and regularizes the trigger-attached subgraph so that its local structure remains close to the original subgraph. Extensive experiments on multiple TAG datasets and representative GFMs demonstrate the effectiveness and stealthiness of STAG. Our code is available at https://github.com/ventr1c/STAG.

</details>

*Sara Malacarne,Andrea Ceni,Claudio Gallicchio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自由概率的确定性核方法，用于无需开展仿真滚动即可选择泄漏线性储层计算的操作参数（增益、输入尺度、泄漏率）。通过短标注序列上的核岭回归对候选配置进行排序，所选配置可跨宽度迁移，在多个基准上以极少的滚动代价达到甚至超越穷举搜索的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储层计算依赖固定循环系统和轻量读出层，但参数选择过程中需要大量滚动仿真来调优超参数（如循环增益、输入尺度和泄漏率），这削弱了其高效性。现有的超参数选择方式计算开销大，在验证滚动次数受限的场景下难以应用。

Method: 利用自由概率论推导交叉滞后传播系数，这些系数总结了储层如何混合过去输入；在大宽度极限下定义确定性时间核，近似有限储层的特征几何。然后在短标注的引导序列上运行核岭回归，对候选操作配置进行排名，无需实例化或滚动储层，且所选配置可跨储层宽度迁移。

Result: 在十个合成时间序列基准上，零滚动选择达到0.772的部署分数（接近穷举搜索的0.774），避免了156,600次选择滚动；在小滚动预算下，所提排名方法在每个测试预算上均获得最强平均性能，仅用4.8%的滚动成本即可达到穷举基准。在ETT数据集上，五个保留候选恢复三个数据集的穷举操作点；在多元蜂窝流量预测中，每单元15次滚动即可达到462次滚动的穷举参考，并优于低预算下的随机搜索和贝叶斯优化。

Conclusion: 自由概率核可作为确定性替代模型，在验证滚动稀缺时高效选择储层操作配置，选出的配置可跨宽度迁移，兼具零滚动选择能力和小预算下的最优性能，为储层计算的超参数选择提供了一种低成本且有效的方案。

Abstract: Reservoir computing (RC) couples a fixed recurrent dynamical system with a trained lightweight readout, but this efficiency is partly lost during hyperparameter selection: the recurrent gain, input scale, and leakage rate determine the reservoir's stability and temporal processing regime and are usually tuned through many rollouts. We introduce a deterministic, pilot-informed selector for leaky linear reservoirs followed by coordinate-wise nonlinear features. Free probability yields cross-lag propagation coefficients that summarize how the reservoir mixes past inputs. In the large-width limit, these coefficients define a deterministic temporal kernel that approximates the finite-reservoir feature geometry. Kernel ridge regression on a short labelled pilot sequence therefore ranks candidate operating regimes without instantiating or rolling out a reservoir, and the selected configuration transfers across widths. Across ten synthetic temporal benchmarks, zero-rollout selection obtains a mean deployment score of $0.772$, compared with $0.774$ for exhaustive simulation-based search, while avoiding $156\,600$ selection rollouts. With a small rollout budget, the proposed ranking provides the strongest mean performance at every tested budget and reaches the exhaustive reference using $4.8\%$ of its rollout cost. On four public electricity-transformer-temperature (ETT) forecasting datasets, five retained candidates recover the exhaustive operating point on three datasets. On multivariate cellular-traffic forecasting, 15 rollouts per cell reach the 462-rollout exhaustive reference and outperform random search and Bayesian optimization at low budgets. These results position free-probability kernels as deterministic surrogates for selecting reservoir operating regimes when validation rollouts are scarce.

</details>


### [37] [RODE: A Radial-Orthogonal Decoupled Engine for Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21024)
*Guoxiang Xu,Bince Qu,Qi Sun,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RODE优化器，将矩阵更新的径向（范数）和方向（切空间）分量解耦，分别使用独立的更新规则和步长。实验表明RODE在语言建模和图像分类任务上优于Muon变体，在1.5B规模下显著降低损失和模型范数，在Qwen3.5-9B微调中全面超越Muon。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代神经网络训练越来越多地使用矩阵感知优化器，但传统方法将条件化的矩阵步长直接加到权重上，同时改变其范数和方向，二者相互耦合。当前范数影响角向运动，而方向学习又驱动范数增长，这种交互关系导致难以精确控制优化轨迹。

Method: RODE将矩阵更新分解为径向和方向两个独立通道：径向通道通过标量规则显式更新矩阵的Frobenius范数，方向通道在切空间中执行Newton-Schulz条件化的更新。两个通道各自拥有独立的更新规则和步长，实现解耦控制。

Result: GPT-2干预实验显示径向范数控制和方向更新均带来收益。在2个语言建模和2个图像分类任务上，RODE在所有直接对比中均优于Muon变体且最终全模型范数更低。在1.5B规模下，损失从4.145降至3.346，全局范数从11964降至2183。在Qwen3.5-9B微调中，RODE在全部四个评测任务上超过Muon。

Conclusion: 解耦径向和方向动力学为矩阵优化提供了更有效且更可控的方法，RODE的成功表明分别管理范数和方向的更新规则具有显著优势。

Abstract: Modern neural network training increasingly uses matrix-aware optimizers, yet their conditioned matrix step is typically added directly to the weight, jointly changing its norm and direction. This interaction matters because the current norm determines angular motion, while directional learning can drive norm growth and thereby alter later steps. We introduce RODE, which gives the radial and directional components separate update rules and step sizes. RODE explicitly updates the matrix Frobenius norm through a scalar radial rule, while its directional channel performs Newton--Schulz-conditioned updates in the tangent space. Controlled GPT-2 interventions show gains from both direct norm control and RODE's directional update. Across two language-modeling and two image-classification tasks, RODE outperforms both Muon variants in every direct comparison and ends with lower full-model norms. At 1.5B scale, using the learning rate transferred directly from the Qwen2-style LM sweep, RODE lowers loss from 4.145 to 3.346 and final global norm from 11964 to 2183 relative to Muon RMS, with fixed-radius RODE improving further. For Qwen3.5-9B full-parameter fine-tuning, all six optimizers use the same tuning budget and the same formal-training and evaluation settings; RODE outperforms both Muon variants on all four evaluation tasks and attains the highest mean on GSM8K and MATH-500. Thus, decoupling radial and directional dynamics offers a more effective and controllable approach to matrix optimization.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 经少样本优化后，最佳评审与人类多数投票的一致性从0.80提升至0.86。对70个保留问题的应用揭示了具体局限性，发现非正式提问不会系统性降低输出质量，反而让LLM重写原始问题再查询智能体可能更有帮助。

Conclusion: 该框架为科学领域中智能体系统的人类对齐评估提供了可复用的模板，推动了LLM-as-a-Judge范式在药物发现等专业领域的可靠应用。

Abstract: Agentic large language model (LLM) systems are reshaping scientific workflows in chemistry and drug discovery, but evaluating their open-ended, tool-augmented outputs remains a fundamental bottleneck. Reference-based metrics such as BLEU and ROUGE fail to capture semantic correctness, while expert human evaluation does not scale to the iteration speed these systems demand. The LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm has emerged as a scalable alternative, but existing drug discovery benchmarks deploy LLM judges without validating their alignment with human experts. In this work, we present an LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation framework for ChatInvent, an agentic drug discovery assistant deployed at AstraZeneca, with four contributions. First, we define four output-quality evaluation dimensions---Completeness, Relevancy, Structural Clarity, and Scope Adherence---alongside deterministic Tool Call Correctness checks. Second, we validate the judge through a human alignment study with five expert annotators, comparing Gemini 3.1 Pro, Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5, and Llama 3.1 70B as candidate judges. Third, we optimize the best-performing judge using few-shot demonstrations of human-annotated examples, improving alignment with the human majority vote from 0.80 to 0.86. Fourth, applying the optimized judge to 70 held-out questions, we surface concrete limitations and find that informal phrasings do not systematically degrade output quality; if anything, it is helpful to have the LLM rewrite the original question before querying the agent. Our framework provides a reusable template for human-aligned evaluation of agentic systems in scientific domains.

</details>

*Yuhao Sun,Zekun Wu,Zixun Huang,Peijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TracingFlow是一个基于Flow Matching的模拟自由框架，将最优传输推广到二阶动力学，通过神经网络回归加速度场来求解动力学最优加速度传输（DOAT）问题，能够捕捉高曲率转变和非线性演化，在合成数据和scRNA-seq数据上取得更优的分布重构和轨迹保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于最优传输的方法大多局限于一阶动力学，假设无记忆速度场，无法处理细胞分化等过程中的调控动量与时间延迟响应，表达力受限，导致轨迹过度平滑。

Method: 提出TracingFlow框架，采用模拟自由的Flow Matching方法，推广到二阶动力学，用神经网络回归加速度场，为DOAT问题提供精确高效的求解方案。

Result: 在复杂合成数据和大规模scRNA-seq数据集上，TracingFlow在分布重构准确性和轨迹保真度方面均优于一阶方法；整合谱系追踪先验后能恢复数学最优且生物学合理的动态结构。

Conclusion: 二阶动力学建模在推断连续系统演化方面比一阶方法更具表现力，TracingFlow为单细胞组学等领域的轨迹推断提供了更精确的解决方案。

Abstract: Inferring continuous system evolution from sparse temporal snapshots is a key challenge in generative modeling and single-cell omics. While Optimal Transport (OT) is popular, existing frameworks are largely restricted to first-order dynamics, assuming memoryless velocity fields. This limits expressiveness, as first-order systems fail to account for regulatory momentum and time-delayed responses inherent in processes like cell differentiation. Here, we introduce TracingFlow, a simulation-free Flow Matching framework generalizing to second-order dynamics. By using neural networks to regress the acceleration field, TracingFlow provides an exact, efficient solution to the Dynamical Optimal Acceleration Transport (DOAT) problem. Unlike first-order methods yielding over-smoothed trajectories, our second-order formulation captures high-curvature transitions and nonlinear evolutions by learning the underlying force fields. Evaluated on complex synthetic and large-scale scRNA-seq datasets, TracingFlow achieves superior accuracy in distributional reconstruction and trajectory faithfulness. Moreover, by integrating lineage tracing priors, it recovers dynamical structures that are both mathematically optimal and biologically plausible.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出一个神经符号框架，将大语言模型作为自适应提议分布：LLM生成因果假设，随后用经验数据对假设（因果图）进行评分；高分的图被反馈到LLM的上下文中，引导后续生成向更有前景的假设空间区域探索，形成迭代优化的闭环。

Result: 在真实临床数据集上评估，方法恢复了所有专家验证的因果边，并识别出专家未列出的额外合理因果关系，可能为针对性干预提供新见解。

Conclusion: 该神经符号框架有效结合了大语言模型的广泛先验知识与数据驱动的经验评分，能够生成合理的因果假设，在数据稀少且领域知识不完整的场景中优于纯数据驱动方法或纯LLM方法，并为不良妊娠结局的因果研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: Adverse Pregnancy Outcomes (APOs) such as preterm birth and gestational diabetes can have long-term consequences for both the mother and child, yet an understanding of their causes remains elusive. Causal discovery in this domain is especially challenging due to a paucity of data and incomplete domain knowledge. As a result, pure data-driven methods fail, and Large Language Model (LLM) outputs remain inconsistent or contradictory. We introduce a neurosymbolic framework for generating plausible causal hypotheses that iteratively combines the broad prior knowledge of LLMs with empirical scoring on data. Our method treats the LLM as an adaptive proposal distribution, generating hypotheses that are scored against empirical data; the resulting high-scoring graphs are then used to update the LLM's context, steering subsequent generations toward more promising regions of the hypothesis space. We evaluate our approach on a real-world clinical dataset for modeling APOs and their risk factors, comparing our results against an expert-constructed causal graph. Our method recovers all expert-validated edges and identifies additional plausible causal relations not previously listed by experts, potentially providing new insights for targeted interventions.

</details>

Abstract: Federated learning enables privacy-preserving collaborative training, but highly heterogeneous client data remain challenging, especially in graph federated learning where clients possess structurally diverse graphs. Existing personalized federated learning (PFL) methods ignore the intrinsic geometric properties of diverse graph structures. We propose FlatLand, a novel personalized federated learning method that embeds different clients' data in tailored Lorentz space of hyperbolic geometry. Our key insight is that hyperbolic geometry naturally accommodates the intrinsic negative curvature prevalent in real-world graphs, while the time-like dimension in Lorentz space provides a principled way to encode client-specific heterogeneity. We develop a parameter decoupling strategy that separates heterogeneous information (captured in time-like parameters) from common knowledge (preserved in space-like parameters), enabling direct aggregation without requiring client similarity estimation and extra calculation modules. Empirical results on diverse federated graph learning tasks demonstrate that FlatLand achieves superior performance, particularly in low-dimensional settings.

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BackDFL，一个系统评估去中心化联邦学习（DFL）在现实及自适应后门攻击下鲁棒性的统一基准，揭示了现有DFL安全研究的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DFL安全研究依赖简化的威胁模型、非自适应攻击者、碎片化的评估协议和不一致的通信拓扑，导致对DFL鲁棒性的理解不完整且被严重高估。

Method: 构建BackDFL统一基准，在多样化通信图拓扑和异质性设置下，对最先进的拜占庭鲁棒DFL方法和改进的FL后门防御方法进行系统性后门攻击评估。

Conclusion: DFL对后门攻击的鲁棒性被严重高估，需要重新审视DFL的安全假设并发展更有效的防御策略。

Abstract: Decentralized Federated Learning (DFL) promises trust-free collaborative learning by replacing the centralized parameter server with peer-to-peer model exchange. However, this architectural shift fundamentally reshapes the threat landscape. Without globally coordinated aggregation, DFL becomes particularly susceptible to backdoor attacks, in which malicious participants implant persistent hidden behaviors while maintaining high clean-task performance. In this paper, we argue that the robustness of DFL has been significantly overestimated. Existing studies rely on simplified threat models, non-adaptive adversaries, fragmented evaluation protocols, inconsistent communication topologies, and ad hoc training configurations, leading to an incomplete understanding of DFL security. To address these limitations, we present BackDFL, a unified benchmark for systematically evaluating DFL under realistic and adaptive backdoor attacks. Through extensive experiments, BackDFL exposes critical failure modes of decentralized learning. Our results demonstrate that both state-of-the-art Byzantine-robust DFL methods and adapted FL backdoor defenses fail under modest malicious participation rates (as low as 15%), especially in heterogeneous settings, while their robustness varies substantially across communication graph topologies.

</details>


### [43] [COEC: Calibrated Orthogonal-Equivalence Compensation for Structured Pruning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21142)
*Peiqi Yu,Nam Ling,Wei Wang,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COEC（Calibrated Orthogonal-Equivalence Compensation），一种免训练的LLM结构化剪枝补偿框架，通过对保留权重施加交替左右正交旋转、基于广义交叉验证调整奇异值、校准Gram矩阵并引入对齐惩罚，在多个Llama和Qwen模型系列上显著超越现有补偿方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练补偿方法（如加性偏置或单一正交旋转）仅作用于保留权重的输出侧，未改变其输入奇异框架，限制了列剪枝后权重的适应能力，导致补偿效果有限。

Method: COEC采用交替的左、右正交旋转作用于保留权重；右旋转在约化Stiefel流形上优化；奇异值通过广义交叉验证选择每层正则化强度进行重缩放；校准Gram矩阵被"降温"以减少高能激活方向的支配；引入对齐惩罚保持相邻注意力投影间的几何关系。所有组件仅需小校准集的二阶统计量，无需反向传播或重训练。

Result: 在Llama-3、Llama-3.1和Qwen2.5系列上、多个结构化稀疏水平下，COEC在困惑度上全面优于现有方法，在零样本准确率上大部分设置也表现更优，且在高稀疏度下增益更大。

Conclusion: 实验表明后剪枝补偿能够部分恢复因列剪枝导致的性能损失，COEC提供了一种有效且通用的免训练补偿方案。

Abstract: Structured pruning reduces the size and inference cost of large language models (LLMs) by removing weight columns, but the resulting output error can degrade accuracy. Existing training-free compensation methods use an additive bias or a single orthogonal rotation on the output side of the retained weight. These corrections leave its input singular frame unchanged and therefore limit how the retained weight can adapt after column removal. We propose COEC (Calibrated Orthogonal-Equivalence Compensation), a training-free compensation framework that applies alternating left and right orthogonal rotations to the retained weight. The right rotation is optimized on a reduced Stiefel manifold, while singular values are rescaled using generalized cross-validation to select the regularization strength for each layer. COEC further tempers the calibration Gram matrix to reduce the dominance of high-energy activation directions and introduces an alignment penalty that preserves the geometric relation between adjacent attention projections.All components use second-order statistics from a small calibration set and require neither backpropagation through the LLM nor retraining of the model parameters. COEC is independent of the column pruning criterion and can be applied to multiple structured pruning methods. Experiments on the Llama-3, Llama-3.1, and Qwen2.5 model families across multiple structured sparsity levels show that COEC improves perplexity on every model and zero-shot accuracy in most settings over existing compensation methods, with larger gains at higher sparsity. These results show that post-pruning compensation can recover part of the performance lost to column removal.

</details>

*Blaise Delaney,Dominic Dootson,Juan Jose Juan Castella,Salil Patel,Andrew Pfaff,Yuji Xing,Jonny Hancox,Karin Sevegnani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Winder，一种利用心脏周期循环结构进行自监督表示学习的联合嵌入架构。其核心创新在于相位等变目标函数与固定的、闭式解的传输算子（无额外参数），在PTB-XL上以约1M参数量达到与SOTA自监督方法相当的诊断精度，同时展现出可读、参数高效且与可测量生理量直接关联的潜在几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理过程的循环结构为自监督表示学习提供了天然的先验，而心动周期是其中定义良好、便于利用循环特性的典型场景。作者希望显式编码心脏相位对称性，以保留诊断有用信息并构建可解释的潜在表征。

Method: 1) 推导相位等变的自监督目标函数；2) 提出Winder联合嵌入架构，将表征组织为相位不变坐标和相位旋转的谐波子空间；3) 传输算子为固定闭式解，由循环几何推导而非学习得到，不增加任何参数量。

Result: 在PTB-XL数据集上采用冻结线性探针协议评估，Winder在约1M参数量下所获得的诊断准确率处于SOTA自监督方法的报告范围内，并展现出相位等变的潜在几何结构。

Conclusion: 显式编码心脏相位对称性能够在保持参数高效的同时保留诊断信息，并产生可读性强、直接关联可测量生理量的潜在几何，为自监督心电表示学习提供了新的方向。

Abstract: The cyclic structure of physiological processes offers a natural prior for self-supervised representation learning, and the cardiac cycle provides a particularly well-defined setting in which to exploit it. We derive a phase-equivariant self-supervised objective and introduce Winder, a joint-embedding architecture that organises representations into phase-invariant coordinates and phase-rotating harmonic subspaces. Its transport operator is fixed and closed-form, derived from the cycle's geometry rather than learned, and adds no parameters. Evaluated on PTB-XL under a frozen linear-probe protocol, Winder attains diagnostic accuracy within the range reported by state-of-the-art self-supervised methods at a ~1 M parameter footprint, while exhibiting phase-equivariant latent geometry. These findings demonstrate that explicitly encoding cardiac-phase symmetry can preserve diagnostically useful information while yielding a latent geometry that is legible, parameter-efficient, and directly tied to a measurable physiological quantity.

</details>


### [45] [Thermo-FL: Thermal-Aware Robust Federated Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models for Edge AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21172)
*Shiva Shrestha,Kazi Shaharair Sharif,Zongxing Xie,Jiajing Huang,Anhao Xiang,Honghui Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Thermo-FL是一个热感知的联邦LoRA微调框架，利用设备温度作为主动控制信号来调节本地适配器训练和稀疏更新传输，同时引入TERRA鲁棒聚合管道，在物理测试平台和仿真环境中同时应对硬件热不稳定性和对抗性更新破坏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调使大语言模型能够在边缘设备上适配而无需集中私有数据，但实际部署面临两大挑战：热约束设备可能因节流而延迟同步聚合，同时拜占庭客户端和通信层攻击者会破坏全局模型更新。现有方法通常将硬件不稳定性和对抗性更新分开处理，缺乏联合解决方案。

Method: Thermo-FL包含两个核心组件：(1) 客户端侧：根据设备温度动态调整活跃LoRA层比例和传输更新密度，在热应力下降低工作负载；(2) 服务器侧：TERRA鲁棒聚合管道，结合了范数过滤、掩码感知方向验证、自适应活跃坐标剪裁和掩码感知聚合，用于处理动态稀疏LoRA更新。

Result: 在大型仿真器中，Thermo-FL在对抗性稀疏聚合下提升了鲁棒性，在干净和攻击设置下均取得最强的BoolQ准确率，同时在GSM8K上保持竞争力；在Jetson物理测试平台上，Thermo-FL稳定了设备温度、通过位图稀疏编码减小压缩上传大小，并在符号翻转/缩放和中间人攻击下保持了GSM8K效用。

Conclusion: 安全的边缘LLM适配需要联合考虑硬件行为、工作负载调节、稀疏通信和聚合鲁棒性，Thermo-FL证明这些因素应当被整合在一个统一框架中而非分开处理。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning enables large language models to adapt on edge devices without centralizing private data, but practical deployments must address hardware instability and adversarial update corruption together. Thermally constrained clients may throttle, slow local training, or delay synchronous aggregation, while Byzantine clients and communication-layer adversaries can corrupt the updates used to form the global model. To address these challenges, we present Thermo-FL, a thermal-aware federated LoRA fine-tuning framework that uses device temperature as an active control signal for local adapter training and sparse update transmission. On the client side, Thermo-FL adjusts the active LoRA-layer fraction and transmitted update density as devices heat or cool, reducing workload under thermal stress. On the server side, Thermo-FL introduces TERRA, a robust aggregation pipeline for dynamically sparse LoRA updates that combines norm filtering, mask-aware directional validation, adaptive active-coordinate clipping, and mask-aware aggregation. We evaluate Thermo-FL using both a large-scale emulator and a Jetson-based physical testbed. In the emulator, Thermo-FL improves robustness under adversarial sparse aggregation and achieves the strongest BoolQ accuracy across clean and attack settings while remaining competitive on GSM8K. In the physical prototype, Thermo-FL stabilizes device temperature, reduces compressed upload size through bitmap sparse encoding, and preserves GSM8K utility under sign-flip/scale and MITM perturbations. These results show that secure edge LLM adaptation should jointly consider hardware behavior, workload regulation, sparse communication, and aggregation robustness.

</details>

Abstract: Surgical procedures such as laparoscopic appendectomy are complex, high-stakes processes, yet formalizing their workflows for decision support remains a significant challenge. Inducing probabilistic planning domain models in this setting is particularly difficult due to the lack of structured event data and the prevalence of implicit actions in clinical narratives, which neither empirical symbolic methods nor Large Language Models (LLMs) can adequately address on their own. We introduce NSPIN, a neurosymbolic framework for inducing probabilistic planning domain models from unstructured clinical narratives. Our method extracts and imputes structured event sequences from raw text using a pretrained LLM, then induces a PPDDL model and refines its preconditions with LLM-proposed revisions, guided by empirical validation. We evaluate the approach on 2,660 laparoscopic appendectomy notes written by 9 surgeons. NSPIN yields models that generalize to unseen notes, and expert clinical review indicates its induced knowledge is largely consistent with surgical practice.

Abstract: Transformer-based tabular foundation models such as TabPFN achieve strong predictive performance but incur quadratic computational cost with context length. On the other hand, subquadratic SSM-based alternatives such as Hydra trade away accuracy for efficiency. To balance both, we introduce Tydra, a hybrid Transformer-State Space Model (SSM) architecture for tabular in-context learning that interleaves attention and SSM layers. Across 30 OpenML datasets, Tydra reduces inference time by 30% relative to TabPFN while retaining much of its predictive performance. Tydra also outperforms an approximately ten-times-larger Hydra model while providing faster inference. The results indicate that hybrid architectures are a promising direction for tabular foundation models.

</details>


### [48] [Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine: Learned Physiological Time-Series Imputation under Realistic Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21207)
*Yu-Chao Huang,Haochen Zhang,Nicholas Konz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAIR（Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine）框架用于生理时间序列插补，采用"先插值粗曲线再多次精炼"的两阶段策略，在连续血糖监测和动脉血压数据上，针对不同缺失机制（MCAR、MAR、NMAR）均取得最优精度，并揭示了低重建误差与临床指标保真度之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理时间序列（如动脉血压、血糖）数据缺失普遍存在，但现有插补方法在该领域表现不佳——简单的线性插值甚至优于所有学习式插补器。作者指出原因是通用插补器忽略了两点生理缺失特性：间隙可能在临床极端值处发生，且间隙长度可能跨越多个数量级。

Method: CAIR采用两阶段框架：第一阶段用双向GRU插补器生成粗略的基础曲线，第二阶段用Transformer精炼器在三次连续迭代中逐步纠正自身估计，使其逼近生理现实。训练采用与信号无关的随机间隙课程（curriculum）策略，评估则按间隙长度和缺失机制分层进行。

Result: 在AI-READI连续血糖监测和MIMIC-III重症监护动脉血压数据集上，CAIR在所有缺失机制下均达到最高精度。相对最强基线的优势从MCAR下的9%增长到值依赖缺失（NMAR）下的19%。此外，仅优化重建误差无法恢复临床决策所依赖的负担指标，而CAIR在精度和临床指标保真度两个维度均名列前茅。

Conclusion: CAIR证明了"先粗插值再迭代精炼"的范式优于单次预测间隙的通用方法，并强调了在生理数据插补任务中应分层评估（按缺失机制和间隙长度），同时指出仅追求低重建误差不足以满足临床需求，必须兼顾临床负担指标的保真度。

Abstract: Imputing physiological time series (arterial blood pressure, blood glucose, etc.) is essential for addressing the missingness that pervades clinical data. Yet modern imputation methods perform poorly in this domain: a recent benchmark found that simple linear interpolation outperformed every learned imputer on real-world clinical signals with realistic gaps. We show that this reflects two properties of physiological missingness that generic imputers ignore: gaps may occur when the signal is clinically extreme rather than typical, and gap lengths can easily span orders of magnitude. To this end, we introduce Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine (CAIR), a two-stage framework for physiological time-series imputation. Our key motivation is to learn a coarse base curve and then repeatedly correct it toward physiological realism, rather than predict a gap in a single pass. Consequently, CAIR couples a bidirectional-GRU interpolator with a Transformer refiner that corrects its own estimate over three successive passes, trained jointly under a broad, signal-agnostic random-gap curriculum. We evaluate imputers stratified by gap length and missingness mechanism (MCAR, MAR, NMAR) rather than by a single average, and CAIR is the most accurate under every mechanism on continuous glucose monitoring (AI-READI) and arterial pressure in intensive care (MIMIC-III). Its margin over the strongest baseline grows with difficulty, from 9% under MCAR to 19% under value-dependent dropout, where generic learned imputers are weakest. We further show low reconstruction error alone does not recover the burden metrics clinicians act on: interpolants matching CAIR's error fail to preserve those metrics, imputers that recover them are far less accurate, and CAIR alone ranks among the best on both axes.

</details>


### [49] [TRACE-C: Rank-Calibrated Relational Anomaly Detection for Multi-Stream Operational Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21251)
*Matthew Faucher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE-C是一个用于对齐多流遥测数据的可审计严格先验秩校准检测器，该方法在2020年英国电网数据中未发现异常窗口，但对Storm Atiyah的检测排名主要源于局部通道而非copula通道，并明确了结果的三个解释性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作遥测数据可能在每个单独数据流都处于正常范围的同时，整体上出现联合异常，因此需要能够捕捉多流联合异常的检测方法。

Method: TRACE-C采用同机制滚动中位数/MAD残差，通过三个窗口通道（最大归一化局部和、高斯copula形式依赖对比、最差标准化AR(1)创新）计算通道秩，再经Fisher聚合后与历史聚合结果进行排名比较。

Result: 在2019年测试窗口中，TRACE-C将Storm Atiyah排名第一，但通道消融实验表明该排名归因于局部通道而非copula通道；copula通道单独仅将其排名第59。对8月9日频率事件，融合检测器排名143，远低于时间通道单独排名40，而重建基线将其排名第一。2020年无窗口被选中，符合记录规则饱和而非无事件年份。

Conclusion: 该论文明确指出三个解释性局限：p值属于选择量而非事件概率；copula形式通道并非真正的copula密度；经验秩计数仅是诊断指标而非覆盖率或错误发现率证明。所有图表均基于已提交的机器可读报告生成，确保可审计性。

Abstract: Operational telemetry can be jointly anomalous while every individual stream stays inside its familiar range. TRACE-C is an auditable strictly-prior rank-calibrated detector for aligned multi-stream telemetry: same-regime rolling median/MAD residuals feed three window channels -- a maximum normalized local sum, a Gaussian copula-form dependence contrast on robust-z residuals, and a worst standardized AR(1) innovation -- whose channel ranks are Fisher-aggregated and ranked against earlier aggregates.
  We evaluate six Great Britain grid streams with a January-April 2019 fit, July-December 2019 development evidence, and a 2020 hold-out frozen before inspection. TRACE-C ranks Storm Atiyah first among 2019 test windows, but a disclosed channel ablation attributes that rank to the local channel, not the copula-form channel: copula-only ranks Atiyah 59th. The short 9 August frequency event is ranked far lower by the fused detector (143) than by the temporal channel alone (40), and reconstruction baselines rank it first. In 2020 no window is selected, which is consistent with record-rule saturation rather than an uneventful year; the highest-ranked frozen window was later interpreted as Storm Ellen.
  Three interpretive limits carry throughout. The resulting p-values are selection quantities, not event probabilities. The copula-form channel is not a literal copula density: the method applies no probability-integral or normal-score transform. Empirical rank counts are diagnostics, not coverage or false-discovery proofs. Every table and figure in this paper is generated from committed machine-readable reports.

</details>


### [50] [ConceptTS: LLM-Guided Concept Bottlenecks for Interpretable Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21277)
*Yichen Jiang,Yueqiao Chen,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConceptTS是一个可解释的多变量时间序列预测框架，利用大语言模型自动生成可执行的概念标注规则，通过三个互补瓶颈（历史上下文、局部预测区间、完整预测范围）组织预测过程，在保持与黑盒模型相当精度的同时提供语义可解释的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多变量时间序列预测模型虽能建模复杂的时间与跨变量依赖，但其表示不透明，难以让从业者理解预测背后的原因，限制了在高要求场景中的应用。

Method: ConceptTS使用大语言模型提出任务相关概念并生成可执行的标注规则，将语言模型的领域知识转化为直接监督信号；构建三个互补的概念瓶颈描述历史上下文、局部预测区间和完整预测范围；共享解码器结合预测的概念激活表征生成预测，支持概念级干预。

Result: 在北京多站点空气质量数据集上的实验表明，ConceptTS的预测精度与强黑盒基线相当，同时产生语义上有意义的概念激活。

Conclusion: 概念级可解释性与预测精度可以兼得，通过LLM自动生成概念标注可以避免昂贵的人工标注，实现透明且高性能的时间序列预测。

Abstract: State-of-the-art multivariate time-series forecasters can model complex temporal and cross-variable dependencies, yet their opaque representations provide limited insight into why a particular forecast is produced. This lack of transparency restricts their use in settings where practitioners must understand and assess the factors underlying a prediction. We introduce ConceptTS, an interpretable forecasting framework that organizes its predictions around named, human-readable concepts. ConceptTS uses a large language model to propose task-relevant concepts and generate executable labeling rules, translating the language model's domain knowledge into direct supervision without costly manual concept annotation. The proposed concepts are organized into three complementary bottlenecks that describe the historical context, local forecast intervals, and the full forecast horizon. A shared decoder combines representations derived from their predicted activations to construct the forecast, making the model's decision process explicit and supporting direct concept-level interventions. Experiments on the Beijing Multi-Site Air Quality dataset show that ConceptTS achieves accuracy competitive with strong black-box baselines while producing semantically meaningful concept activations.

</details>


### [51] [SPARCL: Spectral Partitioned Analytic Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21307)
*James Hartley,Zeropy Surio,Daniel Whitmore,Hannah Clarke,Thomas Reed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARCL，一种谱分区解析持续学习方法，通过将运行自相关分解为高能核心与残差互补，冻结核心子空间中的旧类分类器组件，仅通过递归最小二乘更新残差块，从而解决解析方法中的谱干扰导致的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管解析持续学习使用闭式岭回归替代迭代优化，避免了传统SGD梯度覆盖问题，但实验发现解析方法在旧类上仍存在漂移。作者将这一现象归因于谱干扰：联合岭分类器共享逆自相关算子(R+λI)^{-1}，新任务样本在旧主导特征方向上的负载会稀释谱并扰动旧类logits。

Method: 将运行自相关矩阵分解为高能核心子空间和残差互补子空间；冻结核心子空间中的旧类分类器组件（提供可证明的旧logits核心贡献不变性保证）；仅通过递归最小二乘更新残差块，并可选地加入残差随机投影扩展。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、CUB-200、ImageNet-R和ImageNet-A四个数据集上，采用冻结ViT-B/16协议，SPARCL缩小了经典解析学习者与强表示匹配方法之间的大部分差距，并且与稀疏特征去相关方法（如Fly-CL）具有互补性。

Conclusion: 通过识别谱干扰作为解析持续学习遗忘的根因，并设计谱分区策略冻结核心子空间，SPARCL提供了一种简单闭式更新且具有不变性保证的持续学习新方法，显著提升了解析方法的性能上限。

Abstract: Analytic continual learning has emerged as a strong exemplar-free alternative to gradient-based class-incremental learning because it replaces iterative optimization with closed-form ridge updates. Yet the usual forgetting narrative, centered on stochastic gradient overwriting, does not explain why analytic methods still drift on old classes despite exact recursive solvers. We identify the culprit as spectral interference: the joint ridge classifier for all tasks shares the inverse autocorrelation operator $(R+λI)^{-1}$, so incoming task samples that load onto old dominant eigendirections dilute the spectrum and perturb old-class logits even when old labels are never revisited. Based on this view, we propose SPARCL, a spectral partitioned analytic continual learner that decomposes the running autocorrelation into a high-energy core and a residual complement, freezes old-class classifier components in the core subspace, and updates only the residual block through recursive least squares with an optional residual random-projection expansion. This yields a simple closed-form update with a provable invariance guarantee for the core contribution of old logits. Across CIFAR-100, CUB-200, ImageNet-R, and ImageNet-A under a frozen ViT-B/16 protocol, SPARCL closes most of the gap from classical analytic learners to strong representation matchers, while remaining complementary to sparse feature-decorrelation approaches such as Fly-CL.

</details>


### [52] [Rethinking Expressivity and Efficiency in Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21308)
*Zeyun Zhong,Joya Chen,Manuel Martin,Frederik Diederichs,Juergen Gall,Juergen Beyerer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: E²-TTT提出了一种兼具表达力和硬件效率的测试时训练方法，通过闭式状态转移精确复现逐token递归的快速权重和动量状态，实现完全并行的块级训练，在1.3B参数规模下与现有方法性能相当，并在长度外推（8倍训练上下文的Needle in a Haystack测试中保持90%以上准确率）上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTT方法在逐token更新动态的表达力与分块近似的硬件效率之间存在权衡矛盾，作者希望弥合这一差距。

Method: 在块起始权重处取梯度的标准近似下，推导出闭式状态转移公式，精确复现逐token递归的块末快速权重和动量状态，从而实现全并行的块级训练，同时保留先前分块方法所丢弃的更新规则时间结构。

Result: 在1.3B参数规模下从头训练，语言建模性能与现有TTT和混合注意力基线相当，在上下文检索上表现更优；在8倍训练上下文长度的passkey测试中保持90%以上准确率，训练吞吐量与高效分块方法持平。

Conclusion: E²-TTT成功在表达力与效率之间取得平衡，证明可以在不牺牲硬件效率的前提下保留逐token更新的时间动态结构，尤其擅长长度外推任务。

Abstract: Test-Time Training (TTT) enables long-context processing via continuous weight updates during inference, but current methods struggle to balance the expressivity of per-token update dynamics with the hardware efficiency of chunk-wise approximations. We propose E$^2$-TTT (Expressive and Efficient TTT) to bridge this gap. Under the standard approximation of taking gradients at the chunk-start weights, we derive a closed-form state transition that exactly reproduces the chunk-end fast-weight and momentum states of the per-token recurrence. This enables fully parallelized chunk-level training while preserving the temporal structure of the update rule that prior chunk-wise methods discard. We validate E$^2$-TTT by training models up to 1.3B parameters from scratch. It performs on par with previous TTT and hybrid attention baselines in language modeling while outperforming them on in-context retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced in length extrapolation: on the standard ``Needle in a Haystack'' passkey test, it retains over 90% accuracy at $8\times$ the training context length. Meanwhile, E$^2$-TTT can match the training throughput of efficient chunk-wise methods, demonstrating that it effectively reconciles expressivity with efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/zeyun-zhong/E2-TTT.

</details>


### [53] [Time-Aware Tranformer-Based Prediction Model for AECOPD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21324)
*Weihao Qu,Ling Zheng,Dongyang Wang,Jiacun Wang,Haowen Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时间感知Transformer的AECOPD（慢性阻塞性肺疾病急性加重）预测模型，利用家用呼吸机产生的呼吸数据实现及时预测，优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AECOPD症状变化迅速，需要时间敏感的预测模型，但现有机器学习模型依赖临床和实验室数据，存在不可避免的延迟。为确保及时检测，需利用家用呼吸机的呼吸数据进行预测。

Method: 提出基于时间感知Transformer的AECOPD预测模型，通过时间感知机制生成有意义的患者表征，捕捉呼吸机数据中的症状及其时间演化过程。

Result: 实验结果表明，该时间感知Transformer方法在多种分类任务中均优于传统方法，有望提升AECOPD预测准确性。

Conclusion: 基于时间感知Transformer的模型能够利用家用呼吸机数据有效预测AECOPD，减少数据延迟，为家庭监测场景下的疾病预测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The rapid symptom change of Acute exacerbation of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (AECOPD) makes it critical to have time-sensitive prediction models. However, most current machine learning models studying AECOPD use clinical and laboratory data, which will inevitably cause latency. To ensure timely detection of AECOPD and minimize latency, this paper focuses on home monitoring scenarios where only respiratory data from daily-use ventilators is available. We introduce a Time-Aware transformer-based AECOPD prediction model, which generates meaningful patient representations using the Time-Aware transformer to capture the symptoms and their temporal progression in ventilator data. Our experimental results demonstrate that our Time-Aware transformer-based approach outperforms traditional methods in multiple classification tasks, highlighting its potential to enhance AECOPD prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [54] [Across-Design Uncertainty in Short Pricing Panels: Evidence from Simulated Price Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21334)
*Pedro Cadahia Delgado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏定价面板数据中，基于已实现价格轨迹的条件估计不确定性与其在不同轨迹间的变异性之间的区分。基准模拟显示，后者占梯度提升模型估计误差方差的97.6%，而面板内重采样方法无法识别这一跨设计成分。文章提出三个关键结果：跨设计离散度满足幂律关系，独立定价单元的平均化可降低离散度，Paule-Mandel方差成分估计能显著提升经验覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短观测期的定价面板可能包含大量观测，但只有少数不同的价格变动。这种稀疏定价机制下，已有的推断方法可能严重低估估计误差，因为跨设计（不同价格轨迹）的变异性远大于给定轨迹内的条件不确定性。作者希望揭示这一区分对推断结果的深远影响。

Method: 采用校准到稀疏定价机制的合成数据生成过程进行模拟研究。将给定已实现价格轨迹的条件不确定性与该定价过程生成的不同替代轨迹间的估计误差变异分离。使用梯度提升规范作为基线模型，并通过Paule-Mandel方法估计独立定价单元间的方差成分，检验其对经验覆盖率的影响。

Result: 跨设计成分解释了梯度提升模型估计误差方差的97.6%。发现经验关系sigma_hat约占0.182乘以V的-0.271次方（V=价格变动次数乘以变动幅度平方）。共享价格路径的区域不能创造独立的价格轨迹，而独立设计特定误差的单元平均化以标准平方根速率降低离散度。Paule-Mandel方差成分将同质模拟中的经验覆盖率从0.469提升至0.931。

Conclusion: 核心启示是研究设计应从依赖固定被动面板转向构建能产生独立识别变异的数据生成过程。仅仅依赖面板内重采样程序不足以刻画真实的不确定性，需要跨设计的独立性来获得可靠的推断。

Abstract: Short observational pricing panels can contain many observations while offering only a small number of distinct price movements. This paper studies the inferential consequences of that distinction in a synthetic data-generating process calibrated to a sparse pricing regime. We separate uncertainty conditional on a realised price trajectory from variation in estimation error across alternative trajectories generated by the same pricing process. In the baseline simulations, the latter component accounts for 97.6% of the variance of estimation error for the gradient-boosted specification. Within-panel resampling procedures use the information of one realised trajectory and do not identify this across-design component.
  Three results organise the analysis. First, across-design dispersion is well described by the empirical relation sigma_hat approx 0.182 V^(-0.271), where V equals moves times magnitude squared. Second, adding regions sharing a common price path reduces outcome noise but does not create independent price trajectories; conversely, averaging across units with independent design-specific errors reduces dispersion at the standard square root rate. Third, a Paule-Mandel variance component estimated across independently priced units substantially increases empirical coverage in homogeneous simulations, from 0.469 to 0.931. The broader implication is a shift toward designing data-generating processes that create independent identifying variation rather than relying solely on fixed passive panels.

</details>


### [55] [Asymmetric Capacity Allocation in Self-Refinement Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21345)
*Zhuoyi Yang,Ian G. Harris,Salar Hashemitaheri,Cassie Huang,Yuangang Li,Hyunwoo Oh,Paul Dourish,Tony Givargis,Mohsen Imani,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了自精炼流水线（生成-批判-修订）中模型大小对各阶段的影响，发现生成器和修订器越大效果越好，但过小的修订器反而有害；而批判器的大小对性能影响极不敏感，但哪怕很小的批判器也比不加批判好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自精炼是LLM生成改进的广泛范式，但现有方法将模型大小视为实现细节而非研究对象，缺乏对模型大小如何影响各阶段以及是否需要在不同阶段配置同等能力模型的系统研究，可能导致资源浪费。

Method: 在5个不同领域的基准上，使用Qwen3的6种模型规模和Gemma 3的4种模型规模，对自精炼流水线的三个阶段（生成、批判、修订）进行逐阶段的模型规模研究。

Result: 1) 更大的生成器和修订器通常能提升流水线性能，但过小的修订器甚至可能损害性能；2) 批判器的大小对性能影响非常不敏感，但包含哪怕很小的批判器也始终优于完全省略批判环节。

Conclusion: 模型容量不应在自精炼流水线中各阶段均匀分配，不同阶段表现出不同的规模缩放特性，这为设计更计算高效的多阶段语言模型系统提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Self-refinement, typically structured as generation, critique, and revision, is a widely adopted paradigm for improving LLM generation and serves as a core mechanism in many LLM agents. While the three stages involve different cognitive demands, most existing approaches conveniently treat the model size as an implementation detail rather than a subject of study, which may lead to a waste of resources. Little work has systematically examined how model size affects each stage or whether effective self-refinement requires equally capable models for generation, critique, and revision. We present the first stage-wise model size study of the self-refinement pipeline on 5 benchmarks from different domains using 6 model sizes of Qwen3 and 4 model sizes of Gemma 3. We conclude that larger generators and refiners generally improve the pipeline, whereas an undersized refiner can even harm performance. Second, performance is highly insensitive to the size of the critic, although including even a small critic consistently outperforms omitting critique altogether. Our findings demonstrate that model capacity should not be allocated uniformly across self-refinement pipelines. Instead, different stages exhibit distinct size scaling characteristics, providing practical guidance for designing more computationally efficient multi-stage language model systems.

</details>

### [56] [SDAD: Spec-Driven Agentic Development for the AI-Native SDLC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20341)
*Vu Hung Nguyen,Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"规范驱动智能体开发"（SDAD）范式，将严谨的前期形式化与高速智能体实施相结合，涵盖意图捕获、机器可读规范、智能体合成及多智能体验证四个阶段，并延伸至团队角色转型、量化治理指标及分阶段迁移蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型赋能的编码智能体拥有数十万至百万级token的上下文窗口，正在重构软件开发生命周期（SDLC），规范质量成为自主交付的执行燃料。论文旨在正式化SDAD范式，回应Waterfall与Agile之间的历史摇摆，厘清智能体速度下工程纪律的重新定位问题。

Method: 论文通过回顾Waterfall与Agile的历史钟摆，引入AI代码作为第四生产范式；对比Human-Agile（约2020年）与Agentic-SDAD（约2026年）在工件、节奏、问责与安全姿态上的差异；扩展模型涵盖团队角色蜕变（工程师、QA、平台、产品）、量化治理指标（歧义税、规范保真度、SER、带修复乘数phi的TCI_agentic），并提出混合估算与分阶段迁移蓝图的务实采用路径。

Result: 论文整合工业与学术证据支持AI增强测试和验证，论证合成权威与发布权威应分离；核心结论是智能体速度并不消除工程纪律，而是将纪律上移至规范精度、显式门控和可审计溯源。

Conclusion: SDAD将工程纪律从实施阶段前移到规范与治理阶段，通过量化指标和明确门控实现自主交付的可控性，代表了软件开发范式的范式性转变。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents backed by large language models with context windows from hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens are restructuring the Software Development Life Cycle (SDLC). Rich context handling and multi-step reasoning now allow substantial Functional Requirement Documents (FRDs) and repository context to be ingested in a single workflow, making specification quality the execution fuel for autonomous delivery. This report formalises Spec-Driven Agentic Development (SDAD) as a synthesis of disciplined up-front formalisation and high-velocity implementation: intent capture, machine-readable specification, agentic synthesis, and independent multi-agent verification under human sign-off. We revisit the historical pendulum between Waterfall and Agile, introduce AI-code as a fourth production paradigm, and compare Human-Agile (circa 2020) with Agentic-SDAD (circa 2026) across artefacts, cadence, accountability, and security posture. Beyond process description, we extend the model to team role metamorphosis (engineer, QA, platform, and product functions), quantitative governance (Ambiguity Tax, Spec Fidelity, SER, and TCI_agentic with repair multiplier phi), and pragmatic adoption via hybrid estimation and a staged migration blueprint. Industrial and research evidence on AI-augmented testing and verification is integrated to motivate separation between synthesis and release authority. Overall, the paper argues that agentic speed does not eliminate engineering discipline; it relocates discipline upstream into specification precision, explicit gates, and auditable provenance.

</details>


### [57] [PrimeAgentOrchestrator: Memory-Primed Agent Spawning for Personal AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20342)
*Myron Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍PrimeAgentOrchestrator (PAO)，一个为LLM编码代理（特别是Claude Code）注入持久化记忆的系统。PAO在代理启动时并行查询两个独立的记忆后端（PostgreSQL实体-观测数据库和Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引），融合结果后通过文件系统注入方式将编译好的简报传递给新代理实例，并管理完整的代理生命周期。作者通过2025年12月至2026年3月四个月的实际部署经验，记录了三代上下文传递机制的演进、每次重新设计的失败模式，以及桥接异构记忆系统的工程权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理每次会话都从空白的上下文窗口开始，无法利用先前工作中积累的知识，导致重复劳动和效率低下。作者希望解决这一核心问题，让编码代理能够继承过去的经验与记忆。

Method: PAO系统在Claude Code代理启动时，并行查询两个独立运行的记忆后端（PostgreSQL实体-观测数据库与Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引），采用各自定制的检索策略融合结果，并通过利用宿主代理的配置自动读取行为进行文件系统注入，将编译好的简报传递给新代理。系统还负责管理代理全生命周期，包括信任预置、带错误检测的就绪轮询和自适应终端文本注入。

Result: 作者通过四个月（2025年12月至2026年3月）的日常部署形成了经验报告，记录了上下文传递机制的三代演进过程，以及每种重新设计背后的失败触发因素，并总结了在桥接异构记忆系统而非构建统一系统过程中的工程权衡。

Conclusion: PAO验证了通过多记忆后端融合和文件系统注入为编码代理注入持久化记忆的可行性，但桥接异构记忆系统比构建统一方案面临更多工程复杂性，需要根据实际使用中暴露的失败模式持续迭代上下文传递机制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents start each session with an empty context window, discarding accumulated knowledge from prior work. We present PrimeAgentOrchestrator (PAO), a system that spawns new instances of Claude Code -- Anthropic's terminal-based coding agent -- pre-loaded with relevant memories compiled from the user's existing personal databases. At spawn time, PAO queries two independently-operated memory backends in parallel (a PostgreSQL entity-observation database and a Cloudflare Worker semantic search index), fuses results using backend-specific retrieval strategies, and delivers the compiled briefing via filesystem injection that exploits the host agent's configuration auto-read behavior. PAO manages the full agent lifecycle including trust pre-seeding, readiness polling with error detection, and adaptive terminal text injection. We report on four months of regular deployment (December 2025 through March 2026) as an experience report, documenting three generations of context delivery mechanisms, the failure modes that motivated each redesign, and the engineering tradeoffs of bridging heterogeneous memory systems rather than building a unified one.

</details>


### [58] [Truth Lies Deep: Countering Semantic Camouflage via Latent Intent Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20378)
*Md. Hasib Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了大型语言模型安全对齐的表面性缺陷，提出"语义伪装"攻击可绕过现有安全护栏，并基于早期层潜藏表征中的"危害签名"设计了轻量级探针防御方法LIV，在PKU-SafeRLHF数据集上比标准护栏提升20-50%的检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的安全对齐往往是表面性的，拒绝机制仅在生成最后阶段触发，并未真正抹除预训练阶段习得的有害概念知识。这种架构脱节使模型容易受到"语义伪装"攻击——将有害意图包装在良性叙事语境（如创意写作）中来绕过输入输出护栏，暴露了现有安全机制的深层漏洞。

Method: 通过分析Phi-3、Qwen2.5和Gemma-2b三个SLM家族在对抗性压力下的潜在激活轨迹，识别出通用的"意图视野"——通常在总层数的15-20%深度处，模型对被伪装为"安全"叙事的有害意图表征发生坍缩。基于早期层保留可检测"危害签名"的发现，提出Latent Intent Verification (LIV)轻量级探针防御方法。

Result: 晚期层对伪装攻击的表征与安全查询在数学上几乎不可区分（检测率<20%），但早期层保留了独特的可检测"危害签名"。LIV在PKU-SafeRLHF数据集上比标准护栏在所有测试架构上提升20-50%的检测率，能有效中和零日语义攻击且无需重新训练模型。

Conclusion: 研究表明，安全对齐需要从生成阶段的关键点防御转向更早的层级检测，早期层的潜在表征是检测语义伪装攻击的更可靠信号源；LIV作为一种轻量级、无需重训练的防御方案，为对抗零日语义攻击提供了实用且高效的解决路径。

Abstract: Safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs) is often superficial, relying on refusal mechanisms that trigger only at the final stages of generation without erasing the foundational knowledge of harmful concepts acquired during pretraining. This study demonstrates that this architectural disconnect leaves models vulnerable to Semantic Camouflage -- adversarial attacks that wrap harmful intent in benign narrative contexts (e.g., creative writing), effectively bypassing standard input and output guardrails. By analyzing the latent activation trajectories of three distinct Small Language Model (SLM) families (Phi-3, Qwen2.5, and Gemma-2b) under adversarial stress, this research identifies a universal ``Intent Horizon'' -- a critical depth (typically 15--20\% of total layers) where the model's distinct, pre-trained representation of harmful intent collapses as it contextualizes the query into a ``safe'' narrative. Results indicate that while late-layer representations of camouflaged attacks are mathematically indistinguishable from safe queries (Detection Rate $< 20\%$), early-layer representations retain a distinct, detectable ``harm signature.'' Leveraging this insight, this paper proposes Latent Intent Verification (LIV), a lightweight probing defense. Experiments on the PKU-SafeRLHF dataset demonstrate that LIV outperforms standard guardrails by a margin of 20--50\% across all tested architectures, effectively neutralizing zero-day semantic attacks without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [59] [A Survey on Foundations and Frontiers of Multimodal Agentic Frameworks: Techniques and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20379)
*Neel Mokaria,Rishie Raj,Dheeraj Baiju,Xiaoqian Shen,Shraman Pramanick,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Arda Senocak,Mike Zheng Shou,Philip Torr,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Yapeng Tian,Ruohan Gao,Salman Khan,Sayan Nag,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统性地调查了多模态在智能体框架中的作用，从感知、推理、规划、记忆和行动五个核心模块出发，分析了从文本中心智能体到多模态框架的演变过程，并提出了以模态为中心的智能体分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有关于基于LLM智能体的综述，但多模态在塑造智能体能力方面的作用近年来未被系统性地研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用五模块视角（感知、推理、规划、记忆、行动），梳理模态集成架构（委派式、晚期融合、早期融合），并通过模态中心分类法将架构设计选择与智能体能力关联，同时覆盖多模态智能体系统在各应用领域中的表现。

Result: 分析了多模态智能体在机器人、GUI与网页导航、多媒体内容生成与编辑、长视频理解与检索等领域的性能，并讨论了效率-可扩展性权衡，包括训练与推理成本、延迟和部署约束。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦多模态对智能体设计的影响，识别了关键研究空白，为构建稳健且通用的智能系统绘制了发展路线图。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) have fueled a wave of research into agency: the ability to reason, plan, and act. This effort has produced agentic frameworks that orchestrate perception, memory, and decision-making around powerful LLM backbones. With the advent of large multimodal models (LMMs), these systems can process and integrate diverse modalities, including images, audio, and video, thereby improving their real-world applicability. Yet, while surveys of LLM-based agents exist, the role of multimodality in shaping agency has not been systematically examined in recent years. This survey fills the gap by analyzing the impact of multimodality across the core functional modules of the agentic framework: perception, reasoning, planning, memory, and action. Using this lens, we trace the evolution from text-centric agents to multimodal frameworks, examine how modalities are integrated through delegated, late-fusion, and early-fusion architectures, and assess the emergence of agentic behaviors enabled by grounded perception and multimodal reasoning. We organize existing work through a modality-centric taxonomy that links architectural design choices to agent capabilities. Moreover, we review multimodal agentic systems across various application domains, including Robotics, GUI & Web Navigation, Multimedia Content Generation & Editing, and Long-form Video Understanding & Retrieval. Beyond capabilities, we analyze performance across these settings and discuss efficiency-scalability trade-offs, including training and inference costs, latency, and deployment constraints. By focusing on the impact of multimodality in agentic design, we aim to identify key gaps and chart a roadmap toward robust and general-purpose intelligent systems.

</details>


### [60] [Interpretable Multimodal Classification with Linear Discriminant Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20384)
*Mojtaba Moattari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于树集成（Tree-based Ensembles）的多模态情感与行为分类框架，在保持可解释性的同时提升预测精度。通过将各模态编码为token、聚类降维、树集成分类器分类，并引入修正的特征重要性度量，在IEMOCAP和CMU-MOSI数据集上分别超过Multimodal Transformer 4.3%的F1-mod和Interpretable Multimodal Routing 3.0%的准确率，同时人机标注一致性显著提升（62.2% vs 43.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高容量模型（如Transformer）虽在多模态情感分类中预测性能优异，但其分布式表示和深层非线性结构难以生成可解释的特征重要性权重，限制了在临床情感监测、教育评估等信任敏感场景中的应用。现有方法无法同时兼顾高精度与人类可理解的解释，需要一种能平衡两者的新框架。

Method: 提出基于树集成分类器的多模态融合框架：1) 将文本、音频、视觉各模态编码为token；2) 提取并聚类概念以降低维度；3) 将融合特征输入树集成分类器（LDT、LDF、LDAB）；4) 提出修正的模态特征重要性度量，通过降低二分类中负类的影响来改善指标/标记检测。

Result: 在IEMOCAP和CMU-MOSI等基准上，所提树集成模型（LDT、LDF、LDAB）相较Multimodal Transformer获得4.3%的F1-mod提升，相较主要可解释基线IMR获得3.0%的准确率提升。修正后的特征重要性在提取跨模态显著概念方面，人机标注一致性显著优于默认重要性（IEMOCAP上62.2% vs 43.2%；CMU-MOSI上46.7% vs 32.1%）。

Conclusion: 树集成框架能够在保证competitive预测性能的同时提供人类可理解的解释，修正的特征重要性度量有效改善了二分类任务中指标检测的解释质量，为信任敏感的多模态应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multimodal affect and behaviour classifiers that fuse heterogeneous text, audio, and visual streams must simultaneously achieve competitive accuracy and produce human-understandable explanations of the cues driving their decisions -- a dual objective that current high-capacity models, notably Transformers, only partially address. While Transformers attain strong predictive performance, their distributed representations and deep nonlinearity make it difficult to assign meaningful importance weights to individual multimodal features, limiting their use in trust-sensitive applications such as clinical affect monitoring and educational assessment. We address this gap by developing a framework based on tree-based ensembles that balances accuracy and interpretability. The framework encodes each modality into tokens, extracts and clusters concepts to reduce dimensionality, routes the fused modalities through tree-based ensemble classifiers, and interprets trends using a novel modified feature importance metric. The modified importance reduces the influence of the negative class in binary classification tasks, thereby improving indicator or marker detection. The proposed tree-based ensembles -- Linear Discriminant Tree (LDT), Linear Discriminant Forest (LDF), and Linear Discriminant AdaBoost (LDAB) -- achieve F1-mod gains of 4.3\% over the Multimodal Transformer and accuracy gains of 3.0\% over the primary interpretable multimodal baseline, Interpretable Multimodal Routing (IMR). The proposed multimodal feature importance extracts salient inter-modal concepts with substantially higher human-annotator agreement scores than default feature importance (62.2\% vs.\ 43.2\% on IEMOCAP; 46.7\% vs.\ 32.1\% on CMU-MOSI).

</details>


### [61] [Representation Affects Retrieval: A Case Study of Skill Discovery and Routing in a Multimodal Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20389)
*Kevin Dela Rosa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以Tinycloud这一生产级多模态视频代理框架为案例，研究在提示词中如何表示技能以供LLM规划器进行上下文选择。通过六任务消融实验发现，技能的全量自动加载优于部分暴露，而部分暴露可能产生词汇竞争，抑制正确技能选择——提示内技能暴露并非单调有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的代理框架需要在不断增长的大型技能库中选出最适合用户任务的技能。小规模场景下这一选择发生在上下文中（LLM规划器在系统提示词中直接选择技能），但该机制尚未被系统研究。作者希望理解上下文内技能选择与大规模基于嵌入检索的技能路由之间的关系，并通过案例研究揭示其失效模式。

Method: 以Tinycloud为案例，其技能分为两类表示：工具技能（封装单个外部API或系统工具，作为原始词汇）和工作流技能（编排工具技能调用并渲染模板，产出命名交付物）。系统提示词通过两种界面暴露技能：内联主体界面（完整指令、脚本、模板，供自动加载技能）和单行列表界面（供按需技能）。设计六任务选择消融实验，对比三种暴露模式：全开（全部自动加载）、默认（生产配置）、全关（无自动加载）。

Result: 全量自动加载在每项任务上都能选出黄金技能；全关模式导致执行变慢并产生难以发现的失败；生产默认配置下有一项任务被错误路由，原因是该任务的词汇信号与一个自动加载的工具技能发生冲突，将规划器注意力从列表中的工作流技能上拉开。

Conclusion: 核心发现是提示内技能暴露并非单调有益：部分暴露可能产生词汇竞争，反而抑制正确选择。这一小规模观察可与大规模基于检索的技能路由工作建立联系，本文定位为案例研究而非基准测试。

Abstract: A production agent harness must discover and rank, from a growing library of skills, the one most appropriate for a user's task. At small scale this selection happens in context: the LLM planner chooses among skill representations exposed in its system prompt, without an explicit embedding-based retrieval step. We treat this in-context selection as the small-N counterpart to embedding-based skill retrieval at scale, and present a case study of how Tinycloud, a production multimodal video agent harness, represents its skills for the planner. The harness ships skills under two recurring representations: tool-skills that wrap a single external API or system tool and serve as primitive vocabulary, and workflow-skills that orchestrate tool-skill calls plus a template render to produce one named deliverable. The harness exposes them via two surfaces in the system prompt: an inlined-body surface (full instructions, scripts, templates) for autoloaded skills, and a one-line listing for on-demand skills. A six-task selection ablation across three exposure regimes (all-on, default, all-off) shows that full autoload selects the gold skill on every task; all-off slows execution and produces hard discovery failures; and the production default misroutes one task because its lexical signal collides with an autoloaded tool-skill that pulls planner attention away from a listed workflow-skill. The headline finding is that in-prompt exposure of skills is not monotonically helpful: partial exposure can create lexical competition that suppresses correct selection. We connect this small-N observation to recent retrieval-based skill-routing work at large scale, and frame this contribution as a case study rather than a benchmark.

</details>


### [62] [Nexus: Depth-Adaptive KV-Cache Splicing and Retrieval-Decoupled Tool Routing for Agentic LLMs on Unified Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20397)
*Mustafa Arslan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Nexus，通过INT8语义查找缓冲区（SLB）将工具路由与schema预填充成本解耦，使MCP代理在工具注册表扩展到250个工具时仍保持近89%的路由准确率，首个参数token比完整schema重新预填充快1.66倍，节省约80%的主上下文token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于MCP的智能体LLM每轮都会重新编码冗长的工具schema，导致预填充开销（随序列长度二次增长）在工具注册表增长时主导了首token延迟（TTFT）。

Method: 主要方法是解耦路由与schema预填充成本：使用带校准交叉编码器边际门控的INT8语义查找缓冲区（SLB）通过检索选择工具，并在压缩文本签名（中位数19个token）上生成参数而非拼接KV缓存。次要手段是将编译好的schema KV块直接移植到实时上下文中，但受RoPE相位漂移限制，超过阈值P=256时用深度自适应后缀重解码修复，必要时升级为完整重新预填充。

Result: 路由准确率在250个工具时保持约89%；首个参数token比完整schema重预填充快1.66倍，节省约80%主上下文token；1.1-1.7x TTFT加速（中等深度），深度上下文时趋于持平；锚定拼接输出精确，但离锚放置破坏注意力；参考无关漂移门控预测失败（Spearman rho=0.193）。

Conclusion: Nexus的"永不失步"属性是对输出保真度（top-1一致，D_KL约0）的保证而非延迟保证；所有定量结果来自单一模型元组（Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct Q4_K_M）在Apple silicon统一内存上的测量，定性边界可泛化，定量范围则依元组而异。

Abstract: Agentic large language models (LLMs) on the Model Context Protocol (MCP) re-encode verbose tool schemas every turn, so prefill - quadratic in sequence length - dominates time-to-first-token (TTFT) as the tool registry grows. Nexus's primary lever is to decouple routing from the schema-prefill cost: an INT8 semantic lookaside buffer (SLB) with a calibrated cross-encoder margin gate selects tools by retrieval, and arguments are generated over a compressed textual signature (median 19 tokens) rather than over spliced key/value (KV) cache. This path is depth-independent: routing accuracy stays near 89% as the registry scales to 250 tools - where a concatenate-all-schemas baseline overflows the context window entirely - and it reaches a first-argument token 1.66x sooner than a full-schema re-prefill at a ~80% main-context token saving. As a secondary, bounded lever we transplant a compiled schema KV block directly into the live context. This is fundamentally limited by rotary position embedding (RoPE) phase drift: an anchored splice is output-exact, but off-anchor placement corrupts attention, so beyond a threshold P=256 Nexus repairs the seam with a depth-adaptive suffix redecode that escalates to a full re-prefill. The resulting never-regress property is a guarantee on output fidelity (top-1 agreement, D_KL approx. 0) - not on latency, which can dip to 0.98x before converging to parity - alongside a 1.1-1.7x TTFT speedup at moderate depth that narrows to parity at deep context. Two negative results bound the design: the off-anchor RoPE fidelity boundary, and the failure of a reference-free drift gate to predict drift (Spearman rho = 0.193). All measurements are from one model tuple (Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct Q4_K_M) on Apple-silicon unified memory; the qualitative boundaries generalize, while the quantitative envelope is tuple-specific.

</details>


### [63] [Environmental Slow AI: Design Principles for Generative Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20398)
*Vanessa Utz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从环境人文学视角审视生成式AI中嵌入的"最大化"价值观，提出以环境可持续性为核心价值的"慢AI"设计原则，包括克制、充分性、选择性优于留存、物质可见性和摩擦作为可供性五个原则，旨在通过设计恢复被"无摩擦"默认设置悄然剥夺的人类决策权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI以规模化方式产出文化产品，但其设计本身内嵌着特定的文化价值观；一旦识别出这些价值观，便可对其进行有意识的重新塑造。作者认为当前生成式AI秉持的是"最大化"价值观，需要从环境可持续性角度加以批判与重构。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）的方法，以环境人文学传统为理论框架，在"慢AI"的学术与实践谱系下提出五个设计原则：克制（restraint）、充分性（sufficiency）、选择性优先于留存（selectivity over retention）、物质可见性（material visibility）、摩擦作为可供性（friction as affordance）。每个原则同时包含设计实现层和阐释交互层两个操作维度。

Result: 论文以当前广泛部署的生成式AI系统为案例，逐一阐释五个设计原则的具体应用场景，并论证这些原则如何通过"阐释性反思"嵌入设计来扩展人类能动性，恢复被无摩擦默认设置所消除的决策空间。

Conclusion: 以环境可持续性为核心价值的慢AI设计原则，不仅能在技术层面减少资源消耗，更重要的是通过设计中的阐释性反思层，促使用户和开发者对系统保持批判性参与，从而在价值层面重构AI与人类、与环境的关系。

Abstract: Generative AI (genAI) systems produce cultural artefacts at scale, but they also reflect embedded cultural values through their design. Once identified, these values become open to deliberate reshaping. This position paper examines the maximalist values of current generative AI through an environmental humanities tradition and proposes design principles in which environmental sustainability serves as the core value instead. The principles are developed under the umbrella of Slow AI, a term that already circulates across several distinct research and practice programs. Five design principles are articulated (restraint, sufficiency, selectivity over retention, material visibility, and friction as affordance), each of them illustrated against the current design of widely deployed systems. Each principle operates at two levels: a design implementation, and an interpretive layer at which users and developers are prompted toward reflective engagement with the system. Together these principles extend human agency by restoring decisions that frictionless defaults have silently removed and do so by building interpretive reflection into design.

</details>


### [64] [When Retrieval Fails Before It Begins: Structurally Indirect Prerequisite Eviction as a Retention Failure in Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20400)
*Minkyu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究固定预算下智能体记忆的保留与检索两个阶段，指出现有以检索为中心的范式忽视了保留阶段的缺陷。作者定义了一种"结构性间接前提驱逐"的预检索失败模式，并提出DSGC规则，显著提升了全链路保留率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索中心的记忆方法隐含假设必要证据能在驱逐中幸存，但这一假设在预算压力下往往不成立。作者希望隔离并定义一种发生在检索之前的失败模式——即与查询弱对齐的上游块（前提信息）在预算压力下被错误驱逐，从而阻断后续推理链。

Method: 作者首先给出该失败模式的操作性定义，构建可复现的确定性基准，并提供逐种子（per-seed）的诊断追踪。随后提出Dependency-aware Semantic Garbage Collection（DSGC），一种一跳（one-hop）图感知规则来保护依赖链中被弱相关前提所依赖的块。

Result: 在主实验套件中，DSGC在词法编码器下将全链保留率从0.03提升至0.90；在句编码器下从0.23提升至1.00。鲁棒性检查进一步分析了预算和规模变化下该一跳规则的适用与退化边界。

Conclusion: 保留阶段本身是一个独立于检索的失败边界，需要用图感知机制加以处理。论文释放了完整pipeline和失败事后分析（postmortem），支持对"先保留后检索"这一机制分析视角的进一步研究。

Abstract: Agentic memory under a fixed budget involves two stages: retention and retrieval. Existing retrieval-centered paradigms implicitly assume necessary evidence survives eviction, but we challenge this by isolating a pre-retrieval failure mode: structurally indirect prerequisite eviction, in which upstream blocks weakly aligned with the query are discarded under budget pressure. We provide an operational definition of this failure, a reproducible deterministic benchmark, and per-seed trace diagnostics. Finally, we evaluate Dependency-aware Semantic Garbage Collection (DSGC), a one-hop graph-aware rule. In our main suite, DSGC improves full-chain retention from 0.03 to 0.90 under a lexical encoder and from 0.23 to 1.00 under a sentence encoder. Robustness checks then identify the budget and scaling regimes where the one-hop rule holds or degrades. Our released pipeline and failure postmortem support mechanistic analysis of retention before retrieval as a distinct failure boundary.

</details>


### [65] [World models of environment, agent and joint agent-environment systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20401)
*Manuel Baltieri,Filippo Torresan,Yivan Zhang,Alexander Boyd,Fernando E. Rosas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从"建模哪个通道"这一全新视角重新审视世界模型，区分了环境通道、智能体通道和联合过程三种情况，并利用计算力学定义了这三种情况的规范预测模型（ε-转换器/ε-机器），提出了由闭环耦合诱导的规范支撑受限环境模型和智能体模型，证明支撑受限环境状态可分解为联合因果状态，为理解不同世界模型提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型的讨论通常聚焦于预测什么变量（观测、奖励、状态等），而忽略了一个更根本的区别——世界模型建模的是哪个通道（环境通道、智能体通道还是联合过程）。作者希望澄清这一区分，并研究耦合与支撑受限如何改变模型的规范预测结构。

Method: 利用计算力学框架，分别为环境通道、智能体通道和联合过程定义规范预测模型（ε-转换器或ε-机器）。在此基础上构建由闭环耦合诱导的规范支撑受限环境模型和智能体模型，其预测等价关系范围被限制在现实交互所支持的延续上，并给出POMDP/控制器示例进行验证。

Result: 关键结构性结果表明：规范支撑受限环境状态可分解为规范联合因果状态的函数，其转移结构由联合模型直接诱导；智能体侧构造是对偶的。POMDP示例证明，无限制环境模型可能有无穷多个状态，而耦合诱导的支撑受限模型可以是有限的。

Conclusion: 该框架澄清了不同世界模型究竟在建模什么，以及耦合与支撑受限如何改变其规范预测结构和复杂度，为基于模型的强化学习中世界模型的设计与选择提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: World models are a central component of model-based reinforcement learning. They are usually discussed in terms of what variables they predict, such as observations, rewards, states, latent or information states. We argue that there is a prior distinction: which channel they model. We consider three cases: the environment channel $O_{:} \mid A_{:}$, the agent channel $A_{:} \mid O_{:}$, and the realised joint process $(A, O)_{:}$, equivalently viewed as a channel with no inputs. Using computational mechanics, we define canonical predictive models for these three cases as $ε$-transducers or $ε$-machines. Canonical environment models recover standard predictive state representations, while the other two give analogous notions of canonical models for the agent and the joint system. We then build canonical support-restricted environment and agent models induced by closed-loop coupling, whose predictive equivalences range over continuations supported by the realised interaction. The key structural result is that canonical support-restricted environment states factor through the canonical joint causal states, and their transition structure is induced directly from the joint model; the agent-side construction is dual. Finally, we give a POMDP/controller example in which the unrestricted environment model has infinitely many states while the canonical support-restricted model induced by the coupling is finite. The framework clarifies what different world models are models of, and how coupling and support restriction can change their canonical predictive structure and complexity.

</details>


### [66] [StateSight: Benchmarking Latent Spatial-State Reconstruction in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20414)
*Michelle Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StateSight基准，包含三类程序生成的单图空间推理任务（立方体网对面推理、遮挡立方体塔计数、四邻接连通分量计数），用于评估视觉语言模型从单张图像重建潜在空间结构的能力。结果显示GPT-5.5和Claude Sonnet 5在所有任务上均显著低于人类基线，且格式正确的输出可能掩盖空间结构恢复失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型的评估基准往往将感知、OCR、领域知识、语言先验和推理混合在一起，难以单独隔离模型从单张图像中重建潜在空间结构的能力。作者希望构建一个纯净的、可验证的基准来专门测试这一核心能力。

Method: 程序化生成三个任务族（立方体网对面推理、遮挡立方体塔计数、4邻接连通分量计数），每个任务含300个单图提示，配备确定性预言标签和精确匹配评分。引入可见推导分析（visible-derivation analysis）来识别错误模式，并配套构建StateSight-Steps数据集（900个图像文本交错示例和3,600个确定性中间视觉状态）。

Result: GPT-5.5在三个任务上获得59.3%、33.3%和28.3%的准确率；Claude Sonnet 5获得53.3%、18.7%和7.3%。两模型均无格式错误。30名人类参与者在60个项目上的平均准确率分别为80.8%、68.8%和64.3%，全面超越两个模型。

Conclusion: 格式有效的响应可能掩盖模型未能恢复进行可验证视觉推理所需空间结构的事实。现有顶级视觉语言模型在单图像空间结构重建方面与人类仍存在显著差距。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used for multimodal question answering, yet their ability to reconstruct latent spatial structure from a single image remains difficult to isolate. Broad benchmarks often combine perception, optical character recognition, domain knowledge, linguistic priors, and reasoning in the same evaluation. We introduce StateSight, a procedurally generated benchmark for cube-net opposite-face reasoning, occluded cube-tower counting, and 4-neighbor connected-component counting. Each task family contains 300 single-image prompts with deterministic oracle labels and exact-match scoring. OpenAI GPT-5.5, using the API model identifier gpt-5.5, achieved 59.3%, 33.3%, and 28.3% accuracy across the three tasks, while Claude Sonnet 5 achieved 53.3%, 18.7%, and 7.3%. All final direct runs had zero format errors. A 30-participant human baseline on 60 items exceeded both models on every task, with mean accuracies of 80.8%, 68.8%, and 64.3%. Visible-derivation analysis identified recurring errors in image-state reconstruction and reasoning procedure. We also introduce StateSight-Steps, a companion dataset of 900 interleaved image-text examples and 3,600 deterministic intermediate visual states. The results show that format-valid responses can mask failures to recover the spatial structure required for verifiable visual inference.

</details>


### [67] [Categorical AI phenomenology: A first-person approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20420)
*Robert Prentner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以现象学为先导的人工意识研究方法，将意识重新定义为智能体与世界互动所体现的主观体验，并利用Q-网络的范畴论数学框架对第一人称结构进行建模。该框架将Q-网络视为编码智能体与世界交互的关系性接口，与4E认知方法相契合，为人工现象学提供了基于范畴数学的原则性、关系性理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人工意识研究往往从第三人称或功能主义视角出发，缺乏对主观体验进行形式化建模的方法。作者希望将现象学方法引入人工意识领域，以第一人称结构为核心，为理解计算系统如何承载有意识的体验提供坚实的数学基础。

Method: 作者采用"现象学优先"的研究路径：(1) 将意识重新概念化为通过智能体-世界界面所展开的主观体验；(2) 利用由Q-网络推导出的范畴论数学结构来建模动作与现象学不变量；(3) 将Q-网络理解为编码智能体与世界交互的关系性接口，类比计算机动态状态依赖感官输入、先前状态和动作的方式。

Result: 该工作构建了一个"界面意识"的严谨框架，能够描述将信息处理嵌入到现象学结构中的计算系统，并与4E认知方法（具身、嵌入、延展）强调的经验维度保持一致性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种植根于范畴数学的、原则性的、关系性的、现象学取向的人工现象学理论框架，为后续研究提供了新的方向和形式化工具。

Abstract: This paper develops a phenomenology-first approach to artificial consciousness by reframing consciousness as the subjective experience enacted through an agent's interface with the world. We shift the methodological focus to first-person structures, modeled mathematically by categories derived from Q-networks to capture actions and phenomenological invariants. In this framework, Q-networks are conceptualized as relational interfaces encoding agent-world interaction, analogous to how the dynamical states of a computer depend on its sensory inputs, previous states, and actions. Our work provides a rigorous framework for interface consciousness to describe computational systems that embed information-processing into phenomenological structure. The approach aligns with 4E approaches to cognition by emphasizing enactive, embedded, and extended dimensions of experience. The paper thus offers a principled, relational, and phenomenological account of artificial phenomenology grounded in categorical mathematics.

</details>


### [68] [Who Delegates to AI? Evidence from 53,000 Agent Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20425)
*Hyeongjae Lee,Jihyang Cheon,Lanu Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种衡量AI采用（而非仅AI暴露）的新指标——代理采用指数（AAI），通过分析Manus技能市场中约5.3万条智能体技能规范与O*NET任务陈述的语义相似度，来衡量工人是否已将任务委托给AI。研究发现：AI委托集中的职业与传统暴露框架预测的不同；AAI更接近AI能力而非实际使用；AAI在工资分布中下部及学士学位层面达到峰值，在最受教育群体中反而下降，且技术可行性无法解释这一缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献大量衡量职业对AI的暴露程度，但这些指标捕捉的是AI能在哪里执行任务，而非工人是否实际采纳了AI。需要一个新的维度来衡量AI的实际采用情况，以弥补"理论暴露"与"实际使用"之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出"委托暴露"（delegated exposure）概念，即工人是否已将任务构建进工作流程中委托给AI。将其操作化为代理采用指数（AAI）：嵌入Manus技能市场约5.3万条智能体技能规范，计算其与约1.8万条O*NET任务陈述的语义相似度，并聚合到职业层面。

Result: 三个核心发现：（1）AI委托集中的职业与AI暴露框架预测的有显著差异；（2）AAI更接近AI的能力边界而非工人实际使用情况；（3）AAI在工资分布顶部以下及学士学位水平达到峰值，在两端下降。技术可用性解释了大部分变异，但无法解释受教育程度最高职业中的缺口。

Conclusion: 技术可行性本身不能解释AI采纳差异。受教育程度最高职业中的缺口可能反映工作抗拒预先规范化，或专业人士对编纂速度的自主决定。区分这两种解释并跟踪指标随时间的分化，需要重复测量。

Abstract: A growing literature measures how far occupations are exposed to AI, but these measures capture where AI could perform tasks, not whether workers have adopted it. We propose a new layer of exposure, delegated exposure, which records whether a worker has committed a task to AI by building it into a workflow. We operationalize it as the Agentic Adoption Index (AAI), which measures how closely an occupation's tasks match the agentic routines practitioners have already built and shared. We embed roughly 53,000 agent skill specifications from the Manus Skills Marketplace, compute their semantic similarity to about 18,000 O*NET task statements, and aggregate to the occupation level. Three findings follow. First, the occupations where delegation concentrates differ sharply from those pre-AI frameworks identified as most at risk. Second, the AAI tracks what AI could do more closely than what workers currently use it for. Third, the AAI peaks below the top of the wage distribution and at the bachelor's level, declining at both extremes. Technical availability explains most of this variation, but not the shortfall among the most educated occupations, so feasibility alone cannot account for who adopts. That shortfall may reflect work that resists advance specification, or professional discretion over the pace of codification. Distinguishing the two, and tracking how these measures diverge over time, will require repeated measurement.

</details>


### [69] [STCO: Conditional Neural Operators for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20477)
*Xingxin Yang,Zhan Zhang,Juan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出时空条件算子（STCO），用于解决神经算子中"规定条件"学习问题——即控制或优化场景下，目标时刻的边界条件（如物体运动、入流、力）由查询指定而非仅由观测状态决定。STCO通过流感知图叶（FAGL）和双站点特征线性调制（DSFiLM）向异构骨干网络注入条件场，在12种骨干架构、3种工况和2种预测提前量下，平均相对L2场误差降低31.1%，压力载荷误差降低24.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经算子用于PDE系统未来状态预测时，通常仅以观测状态和静态问题描述符为条件，但在控制或优化场景中，物体运动、入流或强迫项等条件是由查询指定的，无法仅从观测状态推导。因此需要一种能向异构骨干架构注入"规定条件"的通用接口，使模型能感知目标时刻的条件场并做出准确预测。

Method: STCO包含两个核心组件：（1）非学习的流感知图叶（FAGL），利用最终观测帧的涡量构造固定基数的自适应划分，并在区域坐标上联合定位观测历史与目标时刻条件场；（2）双站点特征线性调制（DSFiLM），在算子计算前后通过运动、入流和力三条独立通路注入条件，使用由当前特征驱动的槽级和通道级门控。该方法在12种不同物理和时间输入特征的骨干架构上进行评估。

Result: 在浸没边界计算流体力学基准上（涵盖规定运动、入流扰动、体力激励和形态变化），12种匹配骨干架构、3种工况和2种提前量下，STCO实现平均配对相对L2场误差降低31.1%，归一化压力载荷误差降低24.7%；11种骨干架构的长程场误差降低；每个条件组的干预均产生可测量的预测变化。

Conclusion: STCO为目标条件注入提供了一个通用的、架构无关的接口框架，在不同骨干架构上均表现出显著且一致的预测精度提升，证明了将规定条件显式注入神经算子对于时变物理系统的控制与优化场景是必要且有效的。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as efficient surrogates for time-dependent physical systems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs), but their future-state predictions are often conditioned only on observed states and static problem descriptors. For control or optimization, however, body motion, inflow, or forcing are prescribed for the query without being determined solely by the observed state. We introduce the Spatiotemporal Conditional Operator (STCO) for prescribed-condition operator learning (PCOL), a common interface that supplies prescribed target-time condition fields to heterogeneous backbone architectures while retaining their architecture-specific core computation and context pathways. Its condition interface combines Flow-Aware Graph Leaf (FAGL) with Dual-Site Feature-wise Linear Modulation (DSFiLM). Non-learned FAGL uses vorticity from the final observed frame to construct a fixed-cardinality adaptive partition, then co-locates the observed history and target-time condition fields at its regional coordinates. DSFiLM injects separate motion, inflow, and force routes before and after operator computation through current-feature-driven slot- and channel-wise gates.
