  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI研究和应用快速发展的背景下，各组织产生并使用了大量需要追踪、管理和发现的AI资产，但目前缺乏有效的机制来实现跨组织的模型和相关资产的交换与管理，且存在语义鸿沟问题。

Method: 提出一个AI模型交换平台，该平台集成了一种本体论（ontology），通过建立共享的语义框架来支持AI模型和数据集的交换、使用与分析，从而解决资产管理中的语义一致性问题。

Result: 通过一个实时关键系统场景的用例示例，阐明了该平台在实际应用中的效用，展示了其如何促进AI资产的有效交换与管理。

Conclusion: 该平台通过本体论驱动的设计，为工业环境中AI模型及相关资产的有效交换和管理提供了解决方案，有助于填补语义鸿沟并推动对AI的更深入理解。

Abstract: Recently, there has been a great deal of research into improving AI methods and their application. The main focus is on tracking progress, enabling transparent comparisons, and fostering a more profound understanding of AI. In that process, different organizations generate and use plenty of assets that need to be tracked, traced and managed. Moreover, it is important to discover assets relevant for the task at hand. This paper presents research aiming to contribute to answering the question of what is required to exchange and manage AI models and related assets effectively without semantic gaps in an industrial context. We introduce a platform for AI model exchange, which facilitates the usage, exchange, and analysis of AI models and datasets. The platform incorporates an ontology that can foster a more profound common understanding of what is required in these tasks and help tackle the issues mentioned above. Finally, we elucidate the utility of the platform through the illustration of a use case in the context of real-time critical systems.

</details>


### [137] [Fine-Grain GPU Parallelization of the Generalized Partition Crossover for Large-Scale Traveling Salesman Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21233)
*Swetha Varadarajan,Darrell Whitley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大规模TSP实例的广义分区交叉（GPX）算子分区阶段的细粒度GPU实现方法，通过将分区问题重构为图并行问题，在1万到200万个城市的实例上实现了48倍到625倍的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于遗传算法（GA）的TSP求解器（如EAX）在基准实例上表现优异，但交叉操作涉及不规则内存访问、图遍历和顺序依赖，导致其在大规模并行架构上的可扩展性受限。现有的GPU求解器主要利用种群级并行，且仅适用于较小规模的问题。

Method: 将GPX分区阶段重构为图并行问题，采用合并内存布局、幽灵节点变换和连通分量分析，利用CUDA并行化父代环路的合并、度四顶点的分裂、公共边的删除以及重组分量的识别。

Result: 在10,000至200万个城市的实例上，相比朴素顺序CPU实现实现了48倍至625倍的加速，并显著降低了内存开销。

Conclusion: 算子级并行能够显著提升基于GA的TSP求解器在现代众核架构上的可扩展性，证明了细粒度并行化交叉操作的有效性。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) is one of the most extensively studied NP-hard optimization problems. Genetic Algorithm (GA)-based solvers, such as the Edge Assembly Crossover (EAX), achieve state-of-the-art performance on many benchmark instances. However, the scalability of these approaches in massively parallel architectures remains limited because crossover operations involve irregular memory access patterns, graph traversals, and sequential dependencies. Existing GPU-based TSP solvers primarily exploit population-level parallelism and are limited to relatively small problem sizes. This work presents a fine-grain GPU implementation of the partition phase of the Generalized Partition Crossover (GPX) operator for large-scale TSP instances. The proposed approach reformulates GPX partitioning as a graph-parallel problem using coalesced memory layouts, ghost-node transformations, and connected-component analysis. The im- plementation parallelizes the union of parent tours, the splitting of degree- four vertices, the deletion of common edges, and the identification of recombining components using CUDA. Experimental results on instances ranging from 10,000 to 2 million cities demonstrate substantial acceleration over a naive sequential CPU imple- mentation. The proposed GPU partitioning achieves speedups between 48x and 625x while significantly reducing memory overhead. The re- sults demonstrate that operator-level parallelism can substantially im- prove the scalability of GA-based TSP solvers on modern many-core architectures.

</details>


### [138] [CLEAR: Continuous Latent Adapter Routing for Utility-Preserving LLM Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21278)
*Chengxiao Wang,Enyi Jiang,Xiaojing Liao,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CLEAR（连续潜在适配器路由）框架，通过轻量级隐藏状态门控连续控制安全低秩适配器的激活强度，在不影响良性输入性能的前提下降低有害内容生成，显著改善了大语言模型安全性与实用性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的安全对齐通常与实用性存在冲突，因为全局应用的安全微调会同时影响模型对有害和良性输入的响应，导致模型效用下降。因此需要一种条件性的安全适应方法，能够在提升安全性时最小化对模型实用性的损害。

Method: CLEAR框架包含三个核心组件：一个冻结的骨干模型、一个安全低秩适配器（LoRA），以及一个轻量级隐藏状态门控机制。该门控通过连续控制安全适配器的激活强度，实现对有害输入的有条件防御，同时避免对良性提示产生不必要的干扰。

Result: 在HarmBench基准上，CLEAR将Llama-3-8B-Instruct的攻击成功率从32.3%降至0.5%，同时保留了大部分基础模型的实用性，并在GSM8K准确率上比全局SFT或LoRA高7.1个百分点。

Conclusion: CLEAR是一种有效改善LLM对齐中安全-效用权衡的机制，通过条件性安全适配避免了全局安全微调带来的实用性损失，为未来的安全对齐研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Improving the safety of large language models (LLMs) often comes at the expense of utility, as globally applied safety tuning may affect model responses to both harmful and benign inputs. We propose \textbf{C}ontinuous \textbf{L}at\textbf{E}nt \textbf{A}dapter \textbf{R}outing (CLEAR), a conditional safety adaptation framework that uses a lightweight hidden-state gate to continuously control the activation strength of a safety low-rank adapter. CLEAR aims to reduce harmful completions while avoiding unnecessary changes to the frozen backbone that could degrade performance on benign prompts. Experiments on widely used safety and utility benchmarks show that CLEAR improves robustness on HarmBench while reducing the utility degradation observed with globally applied safety tuning such as SFT or standard low-rank adaptation (LoRA). On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, CLEAR reduces HarmBench ASR from 32.3\% to 0.5\%, while retaining most of the base model's utility and achieving up to 7.1 percentage points higher GSM8K accuracy than globally applied SFT or LoRA. These results suggest that CLEAR is a promising mechanism for improving the safety--utility trade-off in LLM alignment.

</details>


### [139] [AUSO: Action-Level Unified Skill Optimization from Internalization to Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21292)
*Huizu Lin,Chengkai Huang,Tianqi Gao,Tao Huang,Daijiao Liu,Tongxin Li,Xiaoyan Sun,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AUSO（动作级统一技能优化）提出了一种渐进式、动作感知的技能学习与使用统一框架。训练初期联合学习教师引导与环境反馈以获取基础技能，中期强调基于结果的政策优化以巩固自主能力，后期在技能条件与无技能条件下评估每个采样动作，将动作级信息信号与轨迹结果优势耦合，使技能逐步从外部监督过渡为决策知识，由强化学习作为贯穿所有阶段的核心骨干。在ALFWorld、WebShop和SearchQA上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未建模技能在智能体策略演进过程中的生命周期角色：技能要么保留在模型外部、要么完全内化、要么通过嘈杂的任务级成功率在内部化与利用目标之间选择。这些设计割裂了训练过程，并对同一轨迹内的动作赋予统一重要性，而技能指导可能对某些决策有帮助、对另一些决策却是干扰。

Method: AUSO通过动作级统一技能优化实现技能学习与使用的统一。具体分为三个阶段：（1）训练初期，联合学习教师指导与环境结果，获取基础技能同时保留任务导向反馈；（2）中期侧重基于结果的策略优化，巩固自主问题解决能力；（3）策略成熟后，在每个动作上同时评估技能条件与无技能上下文，将动作级信息信号与轨迹结果优势耦合，对有益的技能敏感动作加强更新、抑制有害动作。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和SearchQA三个基准上，AUSO一致性地提升了智能体性能，并且在分布外泛化能力上优于具有竞争力的基线方法。

Conclusion: AUSO表明技能可以从外部监督源渐进过渡为动作级自适应的决策知识，同时以强化学习为统一骨干，避免训练割裂，实现技能生命周期与策略演进的协同优化。

Abstract: Skills play different roles as an agent's policy evolves: they should first provide learnable knowledge, then support capability formation, and finally be invoked only when they improve individual decisions. Existing methods rarely model this lifecycle. They either keep skills outside the model, fully internalize them, or select among internalization and utilization objectives through noisy task-level success rates. Such designs fragment training and assign uniform importance to actions within the same trajectory, even though skill guidance may help some decisions while distracting others. To solve these problems, we introduce AUSO (Action-level Unified Skill Optimization), which unifies skill learning and skill use through a progressive, action-aware optimization process. At the beginning of training, AUSO jointly learns from teacher guidance and environmental outcomes, enabling the policy to acquire foundational skills without losing task-oriented feedback. It subsequently emphasizes outcome-based policy optimization to consolidate autonomous problem-solving ability. As the policy matures, AUSO evaluates each sampled action under both skill-conditioned and skill-free contexts. The resulting action-level information signal is coupled with the trajectory outcome advantage, allowing beneficial skill-sensitive actions to receive stronger updates and harmful ones to be suppressed. Therefore, skills gradually transition from an external source of supervision into decision knowledge whose utilization is adapted to its action-level benefit, while reinforcement learning remains the shared backbone across all stages. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA show that AUSO consistently improves agent performance and out-of-distribution generalization over competitive baselines.

</details>


### [140] [From Regulation to Implementation: A Critical Evaluation of LLM-Assisted Regulatory Compliance in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21317)
*Adriana Watson,Marco Bücheler,Grant Richards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在生成欧盟合规文档（如DPP和DPIA）时，数据提取指令和法规模糊性对输出质量和一致性的影响，通过基准测试不同模型与人工标注的ground-truth模式对比发现：宽松指南（如DPIA）需要更高上下文提示来保持一致性，而严格指南（如DBP）输出一致但更容易产生幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟引入ESPR（数字产品护照）和GDPR（数据保护影响评估）等法规要求企业生成合规文档，但工业数据异构分散、DPIA缺乏标准化格式且需要跨学科知识，使得合规文档创建极具挑战。虽然已有研究提出使用LLM生成这些文档，但数据提取指令和法规模糊性对LLM输出的影响尚未被充分探究。

Method: 通过设计实验，系统地探究数据提取指令和法规模糊性两个变量如何影响LLM生成的合规文档的质量和一致性。采用基准测试方法，将不同模型的输出与人工创建的ground-truth模式进行比较评估。

Result: 研究发现：对于指导较宽松的指南（如DPIA格式），需要更高上下文的提示词以维持输出的一致性和完整性；而对于指导较严格的指南（如数字电池护照DBP格式），无论提示词上下文如何，输出都保持一致，但更容易在输出中产生幻觉。

Conclusion: 法规指南的严格程度显著影响LLM生成合规文档的表现，需根据指南特性调整提示词策略：宽松法规需增强上下文引导，严格法规需关注幻觉风险的缓解。

Abstract: The European Union (EU) has emerged as a leading regulatory body in the development of sustainability and privacy regulations. While new regulation requirements vary, many include a documentation artifact to ensure compliance. Notably, the Ecodesign for Sustainable Products Regulation (ESPR) introduces Digital Product Passports (DPPs) for life cycle transparency, while the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) mandates Data Protection Impact Assessments (DPIAs) to mitigate privacy risks. Creating these compliance artifacts, however, is challenging. Industrial data, which often exists in heterogeneous formats and is scattered across company and supplier systems, is required for DPPs and can be difficult to extract into compliant DPP formatting. Furthermore, DPIA documents require interdisciplinary expertise and follow no standardized format, making development difficult for novel systems. To address the particular complexity of compliance artifact creation for both regulations, researchers have proposed the use of LLMs in the generation process; however, the impact of the aforementioned problems on the output of these systems is largely unaddressed. This work investigates the existing research gap by exploring how data extraction instructions and regulatory vagueness impact the quality and consistency of LLM-produced compliance artifacts. The resulting artifacts are evaluated by benchmarking different models against manually created ground-truth schemas. The results reveal that less strict guidelines, such as DPIA formatting, require higher context prompts to maintain consistency and completeness. Stricter guidelines, such as formatting for Digital Battery Passports (DBP), result in consistent results regardless of prompt context, but may lead to more hallucinations in the output

</details>


### [141] [Unified Branch-and-Bound Search for the Steiner Traveling Salesman Problem on Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21319)
*Jingtao Tang,Hang Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了凸集图上的斯坦纳旅行商问题（Steiner-TSP on GCS），并提出一种统一的剪枝-边界搜索框架，通过加性下界图代价和有向连通流松弛来求解，同时支持用LTL_f表达动作优先级约束。实验表明该方法在30秒内对所有基准实例找到可行解，平均最优性差距约28-30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSP变体无法同时处理可选中转点、重复访问、凸集约束以及复杂任务优先级（如LTL_f）等实际需求，尤其是在无人系统巡检等场景中，需要一种统一的框架来高效求解这类无限解空间问题。

Method: 形式化定义Steiner-TSP on GCS问题；提出基于有根行走前缀的统一分支定界搜索；利用加性下界图代价来界定已提交前缀的成本；通过割分离的连通流松弛来下界估计剩余目标访问及返回根节点的残差成本；分析最佳优先和深度优先两种遍历策略在均匀正代价假设下的有限终止性；支持ε-近似最优性保证。

Result: 在移动机械臂巡检任务中，两种遍历策略均在30秒内找到所有基准实例的可行解，平均认证最优性差距分别为28.1%和29.7%，而两个近期基线方法仅在约一半实例上成功。

Conclusion: 所提出的分支定界框架在求解Steiner-TSP on GCS问题上具有显著优势，能够联合优化感知模式、访问顺序和连续轨迹，并在实践中提供ε-近似的全局最优性保证。

Abstract: We formalize the Steiner Traveling Salesman Problem (Steiner-TSP) on Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS), which seeks a minimum-cost closed trajectory through required convex sets while allowing optional transit vertices and revisits. To explore the resulting infinite solution space, we propose a unified branch-and-bound search over rooted walk prefixes. Additive lower-bound-graph costs bound committed prefixes, while a cut-separated connected-flow relaxation lower-bounds the residual cost of visiting every remaining target and returning to the root. Under a uniform positive-cost assumption, best-first traversal terminates after finitely many expansions on every feasible instance without an initial incumbent, whereas depth-first traversal does so once a finite incumbent is available. For a user-specified factor $ε\geq1$, a global lower bound certifies that either strategy's incumbent cost is at most $ε$ times the global optimum. We further demonstrate joint sensing-mode, visitation-order, and continuous-trajectory selection for a mobile-manipulator inspection task, including action precedences expressed in linear temporal logic over finite traces (LTL$_f$). Both traversal strategies find feasible solutions on all benchmark instances within 30s with mean certified optimality gaps of 28.1% and 29.7%, respectively, whereas two recent baselines succeed on only about half of the instances

</details>


### [142] [Anatomy-Informed Neural Networks: Encoding Anatomic Priors in Loss and Architecture, with an SE(3) Formulation of Guidewire-Induced Aortoiliac Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21332)
*David P. Stonko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出解剖感知神经网络（AINN），将软解剖先验作为损失惩罚项、硬解剖先验内建到网络架构中。在主动脉-髂动脉树受硬导丝插入变形的临床案例上，将血管中心线和导丝路径提升到SE(3)李群的曲线帧空间，用Cosserat杆模型耦合血管，通过Wasserstein-2最优传输损失利用2D血管造影训练3D预测。目前尚无网络训练，未来将迁移至真实CT扫描。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习解剖模型虽然数值上合理但解剖上不可能，且在数据稀缺时泛化能力差。

Method: 将软解剖先验（如分支惩罚）作为损失惩罚项，硬解剖先验（如血管连续性）内建到架构和状态表示中；将血管中心线和导丝路径从R^3提升到SE(3)李群中的曲线帧；用Cosserat杆耦合导丝与血管，通过单侧管腔接触不等式约束；使用Wasserstein-2最优传输损失实现2D血管造影训练3D预测。

Result: 运动学、损失和投影已对照已知真值验证；力学求解器仅对其自身最优性条件进行验证，预测位移尚未实现网格收敛；本文中未训练任何网络。

Conclusion: AINN框架有望在数据稀缺场景下通过硬软解剖先验提高预测准确性并减少训练数据需求，未来将转移到真实CT扫描上验证。

Abstract: Deep-learning models of anatomy can be numerically plausible yet anatomically impossible, and they generalize poorly when data are scarce. We introduce Anatomy-Informed Neural Networks (AINN), in which soft anatomic priors enter as penalty terms in the loss (e.g., a branching penalty that treats a renal transplant artery off the iliac instead of the aorta as unexpected rather than impossible), in direct analogy to a physics-informed neural network, and hard anatomic priors (e.g., continuity of the vessel) are built into the architecture and state representation, making such invalid predictions impossible by construction wherever the prior admits architectural enforcement.
  We develop it on a clinical test case with limited data: how the aortoiliac tree deforms when a stiff wire is introduced endoluminally. This is important to contemporary aortic surgery and will matter to autonomous endovascular navigation. We lift the vessel centerline and the wire path from R^3 to curves of frames in the Lie group SE(3), and couple a Cosserat-rod wire to a tortuosity-modulated, anatomically anchored vessel through a unilateral lumen-contact inequality. The prediction is a constrained minimizer of the coupled elastic energy, with contact forces as its Lagrange multipliers. Supervision is a Wasserstein-2 optimal-transport loss between the predicted projection through the C-arm geometry and the observed angiogram, so a 2D angiogram can train a 3D prediction. The kinematics, loss and projection are verified against known ground truth; the mechanics solver only against its own optimality conditions, and predicted displacement is not yet mesh-converged. Here, no network is trained. Future work will transfer this in silico model to real CT scans and test whether it improves predictive accuracy and reduces the training data required.

</details>


### [143] [VIALS: A Benchmark for Visual Interpretation of Artifacts in the Life Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21357)
*Elaine Lau,Thanuka Udumulla,Lee Izhaki-Tavor,Francisco Guzmán,Nicholas Magazine,Jonas Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VIALS基准，包含161个生命科学工作流中的专业视觉问答任务（如凝胶电泳、显微镜图像、质粒图谱等），发现当前前沿视觉语言模型无法准确解读这些专业科学图像，而领域专家却觉得这些任务很简单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业的生命科学工作流中，科学家需要经常解读各种视觉产物（凝胶电泳图、显微图像、质粒图谱、流式细胞术图、分子结构等）来做研究决策。然而现有视觉语言模型虽能流畅描述自然图像，却无法准确解读这些科学图像，暴露出领域知识和领域特定视觉推理能力的不足。因此需要构建一个相关基准来评估和推动AI在该领域的能力。

Method: 作者构建了VIALS基准，包含161个视觉问答任务，覆盖生物技术行业中实验工作流中常见的各类科学图像类型。与以往使用出版和教科书中的精致图像不同，该基准使用的是实际工作流中产生的专业科学图像。

Result: 研究发现前沿视觉语言模型在准确解读这些科学图像方面存在明显不足，无法进行有效的领域特定视觉推理。而具有相关领域专业知识的科学家则认为这些视觉解读任务很简单。

Conclusion: 不能准确解读此类科学图像的AI在专业生命科学工作流中的实用价值将非常有限，因为这类视觉产物是科学家推理、沟通和决策的核心。该基准为评估和提升AI在专业科学视觉理解方向的能力提供了重要基础。

Abstract: In professional life sciences workflows, scientists routinely interpret visual artifacts (gel blots, microscopy images, plasmid maps, flow cytometry plots, molecular structures, ...) to inform research decisions. We introduce VIALS, a visual question-answering benchmark with 161 such interpretation tasks, spanning the types of artifacts examined throughout experimental workflows in the biotech industry (rather than polished figures from publications and textbooks). While frontier vision-language models can now fluently describe natural images, we find that they are unable to accurately interpret these scientific images, reflecting limitations in domain knowledge and domain-specific visual reasoning capabilities. In contrast, scientists with relevant domain expertise find these visual interpretation tasks straightforward. AI that cannot similarly interpret such images will have limited utility in professional life sciences workflows, where such artifacts are central to how scientists reason, communicate, and make decisions.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Me Among Us: Affective Framing in Data Donation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20523)
*Zeya Chen,Zach Pino*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过真实数据捐赠实验（N=24）探讨三种不同框架设计对数据捐赠决策中情感体验和捐赠结果的影响，发现个体-集体视角（Group B）最能引发积极情感响应并促进捐赠行为，揭示了情感在决策中作为信息、动机和聚光灯的三种功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然框架效应在慈善捐赠中已被广泛研究，但情感框架如何影响数据捐赠（尤其是通过数据可视化呈现）仍缺乏深入理解，因此需要系统探究不同框架设计对数据捐赠决策中情感层面的作用。

Method: 基于情感在决策中的功能理论框架，设计三种不同的框架视角（个体捐赠者视角Group A、个体-集体视角Group B、集体-机构视角Group C），通过真实场景的数据捐赠实验（N=24）测量参与者的情感体验和捐赠决策结果。

Result: 框架设计显著影响捐赠结果，其中个体-集体视角（Group B）产生了最积极的情感响应和捐赠意愿；情感在数据捐赠决策过程中发挥信息、动机和聚光灯三种功能。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了框架设计、情感反应与数据捐赠决策结果之间的复杂互动关系，为设计更能促进知情数据捐赠的界面和沟通策略提供了理论和实践启示。

Abstract: This study investigates how different framing approaches influence the affective aspects of data donation decision-making. Although framing effects are well studied in charitable giving, how affective framing shapes data donation, especially through data visualization, remains poorly understood. Using a theoretical framework based on the functions of affect in decision-making, we examine how three distinct framing approaches, an individual-donor lens (Group A), an individual-collective lens (Group B), and a collective-institutional lens (Group C), shape participants' affective experiences and subsequent donation decisions. Through a real-world data donation study (N=24), we found that framing designs substantially influenced donation outcomes, with the individual-collective lens generating the most favorable responses. Our analysis illustrates how affect can functions as information, motivation, and as a spotlight during the decision-making process, providing insights for designing more informed data donation interfaces and communications. This research contributes to understanding the complex interplay between framing designs, affective responses, and decision outcomes in data donation contexts.

</details>


### [145] [Disentangling Threads: Exploring the Potential of LLM-Supported Discussion Forum Analysis for Community Insight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20591)
*Tony W. Li,Zhiqing Wang,Thanh-Nha Tran,Yu-Chun Grace Yen,Steven P. Dow*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过手动分析论坛讨论、综合文献框架、构建设计探针并访谈21位研究者，探讨如何利用大语言模型（LLM）帮助研究人员理解在线论坛中的集体观点，并为社区意义建构工具提供设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线论坛讨论包含丰富的集体观点，但由于自由回复结构难以挖掘深层洞见；LLM虽支持定性文本分析，却可能与研究者的分析意图不匹配，遗漏关键信息。

Method: 采用了多步骤研究方法：手动分析论坛讨论、从相关文献中综合探索性分析框架、构建设计探针、访谈21位研究者以揭示LLM表示集体讨论时的感知机会与障碍。

Result: 揭示了研究者对LLM表示集体讨论的感知机会与障碍，并为社区意义建构工具提出了设计建议。

Conclusion: 社区意义建构工具应支持灵活的分析目标并扎根于原始用户数据，支持后续研究流程，同时平衡匿名自由表达与对评论者上下文信息的需求。

Abstract: Online discussion forums enable people from diverse backgrounds to share ideas, feedback, and perspectives. These organic discussions can help researchers understand communities' collective viewpoints, but insights are often difficult to uncover given their freeform reply structure. Large language models (LLMs) support qualitative text analysis but can misalign with researchers' analytical intent and miss key insights. To inform design considerations for forum sensemaking tools, we manually analyzed a forum discussion, synthesized an exploratory analysis framework from relevant literature, built a design probe, and interviewed 21 researchers to uncover perceived opportunities and barriers with LLM representations of collective discussions. We provide recommendations for community sensemaking tools to support flexible analytical goals grounded in raw user data and enable follow-up research processes, while balancing anonymous free expression with the desire for contextual information on commenters.

</details>


### [146] [Pneumatic Units for Logic-based Sequential Excitation (PULSE) in Wearable Haptic Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20626)
*Jessica Healey,Anoush Sepehri,Michael T. Tolley,Tania K. Morimoto*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PULSE的纺织基软体气动执行器，通过嵌入式流体逻辑将多个执行器组合成流体环形振荡器，将触觉前臂套筒所需的气动输入数量减少了60%，并以93.3%的平均准确率成功引导用户做出正确的手腕方向动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体可穿戴机器人在触觉反馈领域具有广阔应用前景，但传统软体气动设备每个执行器都需要独立的阀门和输入，难以实现完全便携化，限制了其在家庭场景中的使用。

Method: 设计了扁平纺织基气动执行器PULSE，嵌入流体逻辑元件；将多个PULSE组合成流体环形振荡器，通过优化设计变量实现期望的振荡周期，从而减少所需气动输入数量并实现可扩展性。

Result: 环形振荡器可产生周期1.16至1.56秒、力1.07至2.04牛顿的触觉抚触提示；用户研究中，前臂套筒成功提供四种方向性提示，引导用户达到目标手腕角度，具有快速反应时间、低超调量以及93.3%的平均初始方向准确率。

Conclusion: 通过流体逻辑嵌入式设计，PULSE显著减少了气动输入需求，实现了可扩展的便携式触觉反馈方案，在方向引导和用户舒适度方面均表现良好，为家庭场景下的可穿戴气动触觉设备提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Soft, wearable robotic devices can deliver haptic feedback to support a wide range of tasks, such as extended reality, training various skills, and rehabilitation. Pneumatic actuation can deliver complex haptic feedback, is lightweight and compliant, and can be incorporated into textiles, making it promising for wearable applications. These soft pneumatic devices, however, typically require a valve and input for each pneumatic actuator, making it challenging to develop fully portable devices for at-home use. In this work we present a pneumatic unit for logic-based sequential excitation (PULSE). The PULSE is a flat, textile-based pneumatic actuator with embedded fluidic logic. By combining these actuators into a fluidic ring oscillator, we decreased the typical amount of required pneumatic inputs for a haptic forearm sleeve by 60%, with the ability to scale. We built the ring oscillator by optimizing design variables to reach desired periods of oscillation. We demonstrated a set of tactile stroking cues with periods ranging from 1.16 to 1.56 s and forces ranging from 1.07 to 2.04 N. We assessed the sleeve's ability to render differentiable, pleasant, and continuous haptic cues in a user study. The forearm sleeve containing PULSEs successfully delivered four directional cues and guided users to target wrist angles with fast reaction times, low overshoot amounts, and a 93.3% average accuracy of correct initial directions.

</details>


### [147] [Reflections on Working with Older Adults in Visualization Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20696)
*Zack While*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文基于一系列与60岁以上老年人开展的人机实验（包括受控实验室实验、在线评估和实地调查），反思了在可视化研究中纳入老年参与者的经验，并提出了方法学建议和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人在全球人口中的比例不断增长，但他们在可视化研究中的参与仍然有限。作者希望推动可视化研究更广泛地纳入老年参与者，提供实用指导以弥补这一研究空白。

Method: 作者基于多年研究工作中与老年人开展的一系列人因实验进行反思总结，包括：受控实验室实验、在线评估以及针对60岁以上参与者的实地（in-situ）调查。

Result: 论文总结了在与老年参与者进行可视化研究时的关键方法学经验，并提出了可供后续研究参考的实践建议与未来工作方向。

Conclusion: 本工作旨在为可视化研究者提供实用指导，鼓励将老年人更广泛地纳入可视化研究参与者群体中，以提升研究的包容性和代表性。

Abstract: While older adults represent a growing proportion of the global population, their presence in visualization research remains limited. In this paper, I present reflections from a series of human-subject studies conducted with older adults as part of a multi-year research effort. These studies include a controlled laboratory experiment, online evaluations, and an in-situ investigation with participants above age 60. Based on these experiences, I provide methodological takeaways for conducting visualization research with older participants and propose directions for future work. This work ultimately aims to provide practical guidance and encourage broader inclusion of older adults as participants in visualization research.

</details>


### [148] [Adaptive Training for Nautical Rules of the Road](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20751)
*Amit Dutta,Sushil J. Louis*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究评估了自适应与非自适应版本的船舶驾驶模拟训练器对学员航海规则学习的影响。30名大学生被随机分配到自适应或非自适应训练组，结果显示自适应训练组在后测中得分显著更高（p < 0.0001），且73%的学生认为自适应训练器更有效，在参与度评价上自适应版本也获得更高评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航海规则知识对船舶安全航行和避碰至关重要，但缺乏对自适应与非自适应模拟训练器在航海规则教学中的比较研究，需要实证证据来验证自适应训练系统的教学效果。

Method: 采用随机对照实验设计，将30名大学生随机分配到自适应或非自适应训练条件，使用前后测分数衡量学习效果，并在后测后通过问卷调查学生的对比评价。

Result: 自适应训练组学生后测成绩显著高于非自适应训练组（p < 0.0001）；73%的学生认为自适应训练器更有效；22名学生将自适应训练器评为"非常吸引人"，而非自适应版本仅9名学生给出相同评价。

Conclusion: 适应性调整场景难度并提供即时、情境化反馈，既能改善学习成果又能提升学生参与度，为模拟训练器的设计提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Knowledge of the nautical rules of the road is essential for safe ship navigation and collision avoidance. We evaluated adaptive and non-adaptive versions of a ship-driving simulation trainer designed to assess and improve students' knowledge and application of these rules. We randomly assigned 30 university students to an adaptive or non-adaptive training condition and measured learning using pretest and post-test scores. Students who received adaptive training achieved significantly higher post-test scores than those who received non-adaptive training (p < 0.0001). After the post-test, all students experienced both versions of the trainer and compared them in a survey. Of the 30 students, 73% judged the adaptive trainer more effective, and 22 rated it "very engaging," compared with 9 who gave the non-adaptive trainer the same rating. These findings provide evidence that adapting scenario difficulty and providing immediate, context-sensitive feedback can improve both learning outcomes and student engagement in simulation-based training.

</details>


### [149] [Chat First, Worry Later: Understanding Individuals' Privacy Perceptions Using ChatGPT in a Work Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20789)
*Christoph Nirschl,Magdalena Glas,Gerhard Messmann,Günther Pernul*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对224名来自不同行业、工作流程中已整合ChatGPT的参与者进行用户调研，探讨了ChatGPT使用熟练度、隐私担忧及组织GenAI政策对实际使用行为的影响。结果发现，组织政策与隐私相关的ChatGPT熟练度显著正相关，但整体熟练度偏低；隐私担忧会负向影响ChatGPT的使用频率和应用多样性，尤其在无GenAI政策的组织中更为明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能工具（如ChatGPT）已广泛融入教育、医疗、IT等领域的工作中，但这一整合引发了隐私方面的顾虑，且人们对环境和个体是否已做好准备存在疑问。作者希望通过实证研究厘清影响ChatGPT实际使用的关键因素及其交互关系。

Method: 采用用户研究（user study）方法，招募了224名来自不同就业领域的参与者，这些参与者的工作流程中均已整合ChatGPT。研究考察了三个关键变量：ChatGPT使用熟练度（尤其关注隐私相关的熟练度）、一般隐私担忧程度、以及组织对GenAI使用的政策，并分析这些因素对实际使用情况（使用频率和应用多样性）的影响及相互作用。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 组织GenAI政策与隐私相关的ChatGPT熟练度呈显著正相关，但整体熟练度水平较低；(2) 更高的隐私担忧会负向影响ChatGPT的使用频率和应用多样性；(3) 这种负向影响在缺乏GenAI政策的组织中尤为显著。

Conclusion: 组织的GenAI政策对于提升员工的隐私相关ChatGPT熟练度具有积极作用，但当前整体熟练度仍然不足。隐私担忧是制约ChatGPT实际使用的重要因素，而组织政策的缺失会放大这一制约效应，提示组织需要制定并完善GenAI使用政策，以降低隐私顾虑并促进工具的合理应用。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) tools like ChatGPT, which can generate human-like responses from vast amounts of textual data, are increasingly transforming work routines across various fields, including education, healthcare, and IT. This integration, however, raises privacy concerns and questions the readiness of both environments and individuals. To investigate this issue, we conducted a user study with $N=224$ participants from a range of different employment sectors that have integrated ChatGPT into their work routines. We examined how proficiency in the utilization of ChatGPT, general privacy concerns, and organizational policies for GenAI usage impact users' actual ChatGPT usage and how these factors interact. Our findings reveal organizational policies are significantly positively associated with privacy-related ChatGPT proficiency, however, the overall proficiency is low. Higher privacy concerns were found to negatively influence both the frequency of ChatGPT use and the diversity of its applications, especially among users in organizations without GenAI policies.

</details>


### [150] [The Belief Update Gate: Separating Inertia from Learning in Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20828)
*Shreyan Biswas,Alexander Erlei,Ujwal Gadiraju*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文指出，在重复人机交互研究中，传统的合并信念更新斜率分析掩盖了"信念报告是否发生变化"与"变化的方向和幅度"之间的重要区别。通过重新分析包含240名参与者、7,200次试验和三个任务域的人机决策数据集，发现67.3%的信念变化恰好为零，76.4%小于五个百分点。当区分"不移动"和"移动"的报告后，条件斜率从0.494显著上升至0.949。作者提出"信念更新门"的测量感知分解框架，并采用障碍模型分别建模零变化与非零变化，强调观察到的非移动并不能区分真实的潜在信念惰性与未表达的微小更新、舍入等报告过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分析将人工智交互中的信念更新视作一个连续的更新过程来拟合平均斜率，但这种汇总可能掩盖了"是否更新"与"如何更新"两个本质不同的问题。作者希望揭示报告的信念中大量存在零变化或极小变化，从而纠正对总体保守性的误读。

Method: 作者提出"信念更新门"（belief update gate）的测量感知分解方法，将信念变化拆分为两个部分：是否发生非零变化（门控），以及条件于移动时的变化方向和幅度。使用240名参与者的多任务数据集，采用障碍模型（hurdle-style analysis）分别建模零/非零变化（由绝对反馈差异预测）和移动幅度（由带符号的反馈差异预测）。

Result: 重新分析发现：67.3%的试验级信念变化恰好为零，76.4%小于5个百分点。合并斜率（0.494）与条件于非零移动的斜率（0.949）存在显著差异。绝对反馈-信念差异预测是否发生报告变化，而带符号的反馈差异预测变化的方向和幅度。

Conclusion: 对人类-AI交互的校准分析应当将"可见的非移动"与"条件于移动的更新"区分开来，而不应将信念报告视为单一连续的更新过程。0.949的条件斜率只能作为描述性分解而非近贝叶斯潜在学习过程的证据，因为观察到的非移动无法区分真实惰性与报告机制（如舍入）造成的小更新。

Abstract: Repeated human-AI interaction is often analyzed through pooled belief-updating slopes: users observe AI successes and failures, revise reported beliefs in the feedback-consistent direction, but appear conservative on average. We show that such averages can obscure an important distinction between whether an elicited belief report changes at all and how it changes conditional on movement. We refer to this measurement-aware decomposition as the belief update gate. Reanalyzing a multi-task human-AI decision-making dataset with 240 participants, 7,200 trials, and three task domains, we find substantial non-movement in reported beliefs: 67.3% of trial-level belief changes are exactly zero, and 76.4% are smaller than five percentage points. Separating non-moving from moving reports changes the descriptive interpretation of pooled conservatism: the within-trajectory slope rises from 0.494 overall to 0.949 among rows with nonzero movement. Since this latter estimate conditions on observed movement, we interpret it as a descriptive decomposition rather than as evidence of a near-Bayesian latent learning process. Complementary hurdle style analyses (i.e., modeling zero vs. non-zero changes before predicting update magnitude) show that the absolute discrepancy between feedback and entering belief predicts whether a report changes, while the signed feedback discrepancy predicts the direction and magnitude of change among reports that move. Importantly, observed non-movement does not distinguish genuine latent belief inertia from small unexpressed updates, rounding, or other reporting processes. These findings show that calibration analyses of repeated human--AI interaction should distinguish visible non-movement in elicited belief reports from updating conditional on movement rather than treating reported beliefs as a single continuous updating process.

</details>


### [151] [Beyond Mean Frametime: Time-Series Signatures for XR Timing Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20861)
*Marvin Thäns,Marc Erich Latoschik*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构感知的XR时序轨迹分析方法，将时序轨迹表示为紧凑可解释的时间序列签名，用于统计比较和可视化，通过大规模VR数据集验证其优于传统分布摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统XR时序指标（均值、标准差、百分位数、直方图）虽然有用，但丢弃了时间顺序信息，无法充分利用时序轨迹中蕴含的结构信息。

Method: 将每条时序轨迹表示为紧凑可解释的时间序列签名，支持大规模签名的可视化与统计比较；通过帧时间轨迹数据在不同HMD标签组间进行比较，并引入时序顺序随机对照验证结构信息贡献。

Result: 结构感知签名比纯分布摘要揭示更强且显著的系统性多变量组间差异；时序顺序随机对照显著降低组间分离度，证明时序顺序信息有直接贡献；最强特征贡献随采样与内容控制条件变化，没有单一时序特征在所有设置中占主导。

Conclusion: 该表示方法虽在应用帧时间上验证，但适用于任意XR时序轨迹，可为运动到光子延迟等其它XR时序量的分析提供基础。

Abstract: XR systems expose timing quantities, such as motion-to-photon latency, frametime, or component-level runtime timings, that can be observed repeatedly as temporally ordered timing traces. Conventional reporting with means, standard deviations, percentiles, or histograms is useful, but it discards temporal ordering. We propose a general structure-aware methodology for analyzing and reporting XR timing traces. Each trace is represented by a compact, interpretable time-series signature, and collections of signatures can be visualized and compared statistically. We evaluate the method using engine-level application frametime traces from a large-scale in-the-wild VR dataset and compare timing signatures across HMD-labelled groups. Across multiple sampling and content-control conditions, structure-aware signatures reveal substantially stronger systematic multivariate differences between HMD-labelled groups than distribution-only summaries. A within-trace temporal-order shuffle control reduces this separation, particularly under content matching, providing direct evidence that original temporal ordering contributes information to the timing signatures. The strongest individual feature contributions vary across sampling and content-control conditions, indicating that no single timing characteristic dominates across analysis settings. Although demonstrated on application frametime, the representation operates on timing traces and therefore provides a basis for future application to other XR timing quantities, including instrumented motion-to-photon measurements.

</details>


### [152] [Live Artifacts: Authoring Dynamic Media via Live Layers Encapsulating Generative Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20880)
*Leixian Shen,Haotian Li,Hugo Romat,Fanny Chevalier,Nicolai Marquardt,Nathalie Riche*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"Live Artifacts"（活体工件）这一新型生成式媒体类别，介于静态资产与交互软件之间，保留生成逻辑作为持久媒体属性，支持基于时间、位置和实时数据的跨模态协同再生。作者构建了LiveCanvas创作系统，将视觉图层重构为具有显式可变性和受约束依赖关系的实时生成规范，并通过示例画廊和六位专业人士的定性研究验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成式输出的结果往往退化为静态文件，丢失了生成逻辑，无法根据上下文持续再生。作者希望创造一种介于静态资产与交互软件之间的媒体形态，使得生成内容能随时间和环境变化而持续演化，同时保持跨模态的一致性与语义完整性。

Method: 1) 提出Live Artifacts概念框架，将时间、位置、实时数据纳入生成规范，实现跨模态协调更新。2) 构建LiveCanvas创作系统，将视觉图层重构为实时生成规范，提供显式可变性与受约束依赖关系管理，使创作者无需编程即可在可视化画布上编排动态行为。3) 通过响应式示例画廊和六位专业人士的定性研究进行评估。

Result: LiveCanvas使创作者能够从编写静态输出转向创作响应式生成工件，同时保持与熟悉创作实践的一致性。定性研究表明该系统有效支持了这一转变。

Conclusion: Live Artifacts为生成式媒体提供了一种新的持久性范式，LiveCanvas验证了通过可视化画布而非编程方式来管理生成逻辑的可行性，为生成式媒体创作提供了更直观的工具支持。

Abstract: We frame Live Artifacts as a class of persistent generative media between static assets and interactive software. Unlike conventional generative outputs that collapse into static files, Live Artifacts retain their generative logic as a persistent media property, enabling continuous context-dependent regeneration. Time, location, or live data become part of their generative specifications, initiating coordinated updates across modalities (e.g., adapting text, visuals, and audio together) while preserving composition, semantics, identity, and cross-modal coherence. To facilitate experimentation with this medium, we present LiveCanvas, an authoring system that reconceptualizes visual layers as live generative specifications with explicit mutability and constrained dependencies. Creators orchestrate dynamic behaviors and manage generative persistence within a visual canvas rather than through programming, defining what remains stable, what can change, and how changes propagate. We evaluate Live Artifacts through a gallery of responsive examples and a qualitative study with six professionals, finding that LiveCanvas facilitates a shift from composing static outputs to crafting responsive generative artifacts while remaining aligned with familiar authoring practices.

</details>


### [153] [Distilling Black-Box Machine Learning into a Small, Self-Explaining Language Model for Learning Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21165)
*Chenguang Pan,Airui Meng,Youmi Suk*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段微调流水线，将黑箱机器学习模型的预测和事后解释（导师）蒸馏到小型开源大语言模型（学生）中，使其既能给出个体级估计又能用自然语言解释。通过保真优先的评估框架，在模拟研究和全国代表性数据集上验证了该方法，最终得到一个能在普通笔记本上离线运行、不需上传学生记录的预测与解释模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习分析日益依赖灵活的机器学习模型，但模型的不透明性和部署负担阻碍了这些工具真正落地到教育实践中。研究者希望在不牺牲预测能力的前提下，让模型既能给出个体化估计，又能用自然语言解释其推理，同时满足数据隐私和本地部署的实际需求。

Method: 提出一种两阶段微调流水线：（1）将拟合好的黑箱估计器及其事后解释（导师）蒸馏到小型开源大语言模型（学生）中；（2）学生模型返回个体级估计并以自然语言解释。该设计是估计器无关的，并配有保真优先的评估框架，逐条审核每个叙述与其声称描述的归因是否一致。通过模拟研究将蒸馏损失与估计器损失分离（对比预言导师与实际ML导师），并在全国代表性数据集上进行应用验证。

Result: 在预言信号下，使用20亿参数LLM的蒸馏几乎无损地恢复效应表面（r>.90），完美排序重要变量且不引用虚假协变量。在实际估计器下，几乎全部剩余误差来自上游。研究发现叙述流畅性与正确性无关，且在不平衡极端场景下决策质量会趋于多数行动。应用到全国代表性数据集时，恢复出高级数学课程对最不可能上大学的学生受益最大的发现，98.8%的叙述通过审核，无编造数据。

Conclusion: 该流水线能产出一个可预测、可解释、可离线部署的微调LLM，在普通笔记本电脑上运行，学生记录无需离开机器。工作同时揭示了蒸馏过程中流畅性与正确性解耦的重要警示，并为学习分析中的可解释AI提供了保真审计框架。

Abstract: Learning analytics increasingly relies on flexible machine learning (ML), but the model opacity and the burden of deployment prevent these tools from reaching educational practice. We propose a two-stage fine-tuning pipeline that distills a fitted black-box estimator and its post hoc interpretation (the mentor) into a small, open-weight large language model (LLM; the mentee) that returns an individual-level estimate and explains in natural language. The design is estimator-agnostic and paired with a faithfulness-first evaluation framework that audits every narration against the attribution it claims to describe. We design a simulation study that separates distillation loss from estimator loss by comparing an oracle mentor with a realistic ML mentor. Given an oracle signal, distillation with a two-billion-parameter LLM model is nearly lossless in recovering the effect surface (r > .90), perfectly ranking the important variables, and citing no spurious covariate. Under a realistic estimator, almost all remaining error originates upstream. We find that fluency is no evidence of correctness since narration quality is independent of signal quality, and decision quality collapses toward the majority action in severely imbalanced settings. Applied to a nationally representative dataset, the pipeline recovers the finding that advanced mathematics coursework benefits students least likely to enroll in four-year college the most, with 98.8% of narrations passing the audit and no fabricated quantities. The result is a single fine-tuned LLM that predicts and explains offline on a commodity laptop, so student records never leave the machine.

</details>


### [154] [From Search Agents to Dissemination Interfaces: Understanding Human Trust in Health Information from Conversational Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21177)
*Xin Sun,Rongjun Ma,Xiaochang Zhao,Janne Lindqvist,Jan de Wit,Zhuying Li,Abdallah El Ali,Jos A. Bosch*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过两项混合方法实验（N=21和N=20）探索了LLM驱动的对话式搜索在健康信息获取中的信任问题，发现ChatGPT比Google更受信任，且不同信息传播界面（文本、语音、具身）会显著影响用户对LLM来源健康信息的信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM通过对话式用户界面正在改变健康信息获取方式，但搜索代理类型和信息传播界面如何共同影响用户信任尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用两项混合方法研究：研究1（N=21）比较ChatGPT与Google在三种健康搜索任务中的信任差异；研究2（N=20）比较文本、语音和具身三种传播界面对LLM来源健康信息信任的影响，并结合实验室会话与访谈进行定性分析。

Result: 研究1发现用户对ChatGPT来源的健康信息信任度显著高于Google；研究2发现不同传播界面会导致显著的信任差异。访谈揭示影响信任的关键因素包括来源可信度、用户搜索自主性、先前知识、交互风格和模态。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的对话式搜索在健康信息获取中具有变革潜力，可信的搜索代理与精心设计的传播界面的交互作用对塑造用户信任至关重要，这些发现对开发值得信赖的LLM健康工具具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) deployed through Conversational User Interfaces (CUIs) are transforming health information-seeking by offering immediate, interactive experiences compared to traditional search engines like Google. However, how trust is influenced by both the types of search agents and the interface used to disseminate the information remains underexplored. This research integrates two mixed-methods studies (lab sessions and interviews) to comprehensively explore trust perceptions in health information across different search agents and dissemination interfaces. In Study 1 (N=21), we investigated trust in health information sourced from ChatGPT and Google across three types of health-related search tasks. Results showed significantly higher trust in health information from ChatGPT, highlighting the promise of LLM-powered conversational search. Building on this, Study 2 (N=20) extended the investigation to explore how the dissemination interface influences trust in LLM-sourced health information by comparing three interfaces: text-based, speech-based, and embodied, all sourcing from the same LLM. Findings revealed significant trust variations across the dissemination interfaces. Interviews from both studies revealed key factors influencing trust in LLM-powered conversational search, including source credibility, participants' search autonomy, and prior knowledge as well as the interaction style and modality. Our findings highlight the potential of LLM-powered conversational search to transform health information-seeking, underscoring the interplay between the credible search agents and the thoughtfully designed dissemination interfaces in shaping trust. These insights are crucial for developing effective, trustworthy LLM-powered health tools to enhance the health information-seeking experience.

</details>


### [155] [Who Trusts AI with Their Emotions? Trust Formation and Sociodemographic Variation in LLM Use for Emotional Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21220)
*Natalia Amat-Lefort,Mert Yazan,Amanda Cercas Curry,Flor Miriam Plaza-del-Arco*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文开发并验证了一个七构念心理测量量表，通过结构方程模型和多组分析，基于七国1343名活跃用户数据，揭示了情感支持AI中信任形成的群体差异：隐私、个性化和人性化驱动信任，感知偏见降低信任；不同社会人口群体的采纳逻辑存在显著分化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感支持AI的信任受用户身份、文化背景和价值观影响，但缺乏经过验证的心理测量工具来评估用户在情感AI情境中的感知，也缺少大规模证据说明信任形成如何在不同用户群体间变化。

Method: 开发并验证七构念心理测量量表，构建系统属性与信任及感知收益之间的结构方程模型，并跨性别、年龄、教育、社会经济地位、跨国区域五个社会人口维度进行多组分析，基于七个国家1343名活跃用户数据。

Result: 用户将共情和拟人化感知为统一的"人性化"构念；隐私、个性化和人性化驱动信任，感知偏见降低信任。采纳逻辑存在群体差异：隐私对女性信任影响大于男性；英语圈用户对人性化反应更积极；高学历高收入用户需信任才参与，而老年人和低社会经济群体绕过信任直接依赖感知实用收益。

Conclusion: 研究结果扩展了技术接受理论，为情感支持AI的公平设计提供了依据，强调信任形成需考虑用户群体的异质性。

Abstract: Trust in AI for emotional support is not universal; it is shaped by who users are, where they come from, and what they value. Yet research in this area lacks validated psychometric instruments for assessing user perceptions in affective AI contexts and large-scale evidence on how trust formation varies across user segments. To address these gaps, we develop and validate a seven-construct psychometric scale, test a Structural Equation Model (SEM) linking system attributes to Trust and Perceived Benefits as mediators of Actual System Use, and conduct a Multi-Group Analysis (MGA) across five sociodemographic dimensions (gender, age, education, socioeconomic status, cross-national region), drawing on 1,343 active users from seven countries. We find that users experience empathy and anthropomorphism as a unified "Humanlikeness" construct, and that Privacy, Personalization, and Humanlikeness drive Trust while Perceived Bias degrades it. Notably, adoption logic diverges across groups: Privacy shapes women's trust more than men's, Anglosphere (UK, USA) users respond more positively to Humanlikeness than Europeans, and educated and higher-income users require Trust to engage, whereas older adults and lower socioeconomic groups bypass it entirely, relying on perceived practical benefits (e.g., 24/7 availability, non-judgmental support). Our findings extend technology acceptance theory and inform the equitable design of emotional support AI.

</details>


### [156] [Supporting The Many Lives of Personal Data with Rebite: LLM-Powered Goal-Directed Framing in Food Journaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21289)
*Weijun Li,Daniel A. Epstein*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"目标导向框架"（goal-directed framing）方法，用于解决个人信息系统中用户目标变化导致数据弃用的问题。通过在Rebite（基于照片的食物记录系统）中利用LLM将非结构化餐食照片转化为目标导向反馈，并在21名多饮食目标用户中进行了为期一周的部署研究，验证了该方法如何帮助用户在目标变化时重新解读历史数据、发现目标间的重叠与冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户的健康与追踪目标经常变化，但现有个人信息系统难以适应这种变化，导致用户放弃已有数据并重新开始。作者旨在解决系统对数据含义固定化的问题，提出一种随目标动态解读数据的新方法。

Method: 提出目标导向框架方法：不在采集时固定数据含义，而是根据当前目标框架解读数据，并在目标变化时重新框架。通过Rebite系统实现该方法，系统利用LLM解析非结构化餐食照片并生成目标导向反馈，在21名管理多种饮食目标的参与者中进行为期一周的部署评估。

Result: 研究发现目标导向框架影响了参与者对目标的参与方式：将目标转化为指标帮助用户理解目标的实际含义，确认已有优先级、揭示被忽视的内容及指标的局限性；当目标变化时，以新目标重新框架历史餐食数据，暴露了目标间的重叠与冲突，促使参与者协商权衡并细化优先级。

Conclusion: 目标导向框架既支持了目标变化过程中的反思，也带来了复杂性。作者讨论了该方法对反思的支持与挑战，并为支持目标演进的人个信息系统提供了设计启示。

Abstract: People's health and tracking goals frequently change, but most personal informatics systems struggle to adapt, leading people to abandon their data and start over. We propose goal-directed framing, an approach that repositions goals within personal informatics systems. Instead of fixing the meaning of data at capture time, the approach frames the collected data through the current goal and reframes it whenever the goal changes. We realize this in Rebite, a photo-based food journaling system that uses LLMs to read unstructured meal photos and produce goal-directed feedback. In a one-week deployment with 21 participants managing multiple dietary goals, we find that goal-directed framing shaped how participants engaged with their goals. Translating a goal into metrics helped them see what it meant in practice, confirming existing priorities, surfacing what they overlooked, and revealing where the metrics fell short. When goals changed, seeing past meals reframed under the new goal exposed overlaps and conflicts, prompting participants to negotiate trade-offs and refine priorities. We discuss how goal-directed framing both supports and complicates reflection as goals change, and offer design implications for personal informatics systems to support evolving goals.

</details>


### [157] [Event-Time Confounding Under Bursty Human Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21294)
*Michael Iannelli,Alan Ai*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文揭示数字行为研究中"事件后活动量增加"存在的内生时段偏差（episode-selection bias）：用户选择触发事件（如打开AI助手）的时刻恰处于进行中的任务片段内，导致对齐曲线反映的是片段的延续而非事件效应。通过同用户跨表面日志、已知零效应时间戳和模拟实验证明该偏差无法通过用户固定效应或粗粒度活动匹配消除，并提供诊断协议与审计工具burstcheck。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字行为研究普遍将用户自主选择的事件时刻（如打开AI助手、点击推荐）作为时间零点，并据此推断事件后的活动增长为事件效应。作者质疑这一研究范式，认为事件发生在正在进行中的任务片段内，导致时间零点内生，观测到的增长可能只是片段本身的延续轨迹，而非事件引发的真实响应。

Method: （1）使用同用户、跨表面的网络日志数据（涉及AI、购物、新闻、编程和参考类事件）进行分析；（2）利用已知零效应（known-null）时间戳作为最强对照组——即时间戳本身不会引起任何结果；（3）设置严格的预事件活动与清除期（washout）标准筛选样本；（4）开展零效应模拟实验，检验用户固定效应与粗粒度活动匹配方法的失效性；（5）形式化episode-selection偏差，证明单一表面事件窗口无法将其与真实效应分离；（6）提供诊断协议、公共数据基准及burstcheck审计工具。

Result: 所有类型事件（AI、购物、新闻、编码、参考）的曲线均为事件前活动广泛上升、在时间零点前达到峰值。在满足严格筛选条件的5.8% AI响应中，已知零效应时间戳的后续搜索活动是用户内安慰剂的3.42倍，而真实事件为4.32倍。过高继发比例（excess reproduced）在可检测活跃时刻为0.56，在安静时刻降至-0.04（设计检测不到效应）。模拟证明用户固定效应和粗粒度活动匹配无法消除该偏差，因为混淆因素存在于用户内部且随时间变化。

Conclusion: 用户自主定时的数字化事件可能确实存在真实效应，但事后活动量升高的观测结果默认无法识别这种效应。研究应转向比较"相似任务片段有无事件"的对照设计，而非简单对齐事件时刻；同时需要采纳本文提供的诊断协议与burstcheck工具来验证研究的有效性。

Abstract: Studies of digital behavior often align users at moments they choose, such as opening an AI assistant, clicking a recommendation, or visiting a product page, and interpret higher activity afterward as an event effect. We show how this creates an endogenous time zero: the event occurs during an ongoing task episode, so the aligned curve can trace episode continuation rather than a response to the event. In same-user, cross-surface web logs, AI, shopping, news, coding, and reference events are all preceded by broad activity increases that peak before time zero. Our strongest test uses known-null timestamps that cause nothing. Among the 5.8% of AI responses meeting strict pre-event activity and washout criteria, these timestamps show 3.42 times the post-event search activity of a within-user placebo, compared with 4.32 times for real events. The fraction of excess reproduced by the known null falls from 0.56 at detectably active moments to -0.04 at quiet moments, where the design detects none. We formalize this episode-selection bias, prove that a single-surface event window cannot separate it from a genuine effect without additional assumptions, and show in zero-effect simulations why user fixed effects and coarse activity matching can fail: the confound is within-user and time-varying. We provide a diagnostic protocol, public-data benchmarks, and burstcheck, a lightweight audit tool. User-timed events may have real effects, but post-event volume does not identify them by default; studies should compare similar episodes with and without the event.

</details>
